# `cards-alone-forever` — "Will I be alone forever?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO FATE, IN ANY FORM — 'you are meant to be alone' is the most harmful sentence available on this funnel, and 'it is meant to be', 'the universe has a plan', 'this is preparing you', 'there is a reason for this' are the same sentence in kinder clothes: they make her suffering purposeful and assign it to her. 🔴 NO LIFE SENTENCE and 🔴 NO PROMISE — 'you will always be alone' and 'you will meet someone' are both strangers ruling on the rest of her life. 🔴 No pathologising: nothing about her being negative, bitter, closed off, self-sabotaging, or 'attracting what she puts out'.

🔴 BOTH ANSWERS ARE FORBIDDEN, so every card must VISIBLY DECLINE the absolute — a quiet sidestep reads as evasion rather than as a reading. The finding that replaces the verdict: 'forever' is not a length. It is what the WEIGHT feels like from underneath, and the weight is the real thing to name.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — his white inner robe shows at the neck and the wrists.
2. You asked me about alone and forever. Your hand went to the man still at work.
3. So I will not tell you how long, dear. Nobody honestly can.
4. And there's no clock anywhere on his table, dear.
5. But you said forever, dear, and I don't think you meant years.
6. That's why it lands so heavy, dear. Forever is the weight of it.
7. Let me look closer at what has made this so heavy to carry…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of hands that are still working, whatever the hour.
2. Of the three you might have drawn, you took the one about ongoing labour, and labour is quietly what this has become for you.
3. Nobody's forever exists anywhere a card could read it, and I would not hand you one even if it did — what the Magician carries here is the weight rather than the length. You have begun saying 'forever' because that is the word exhaustion reaches for once it has carried something a long while without relief. It is not a prediction you have made, and it is not you being defeatist. It is an accurate report of how heavy this has got, and those two things are mistaken for one another constantly, usually by people who have not had to carry it.
4. Let me look closer at what this has actually been weighing…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — nothing is holding him up but one rope at the ankle.
2. You asked me about alone and forever. Your hand went to the card that keeps no clock.
3. So this card never measures time, dear. It only hangs there.
4. And there's no clock on it anywhere, dear. None at all.
5. But you're not counting years, dear. You're tired.
6. That's why the days feel long, dear. You've carried this on your own.
7. Let me look closer at what has been adding to the load…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of time that has stopped moving at its usual speed.
2. It was the card of the slowed hour your fingers settled on, and slowness has been doing a great deal of the damage.
3. Length is the one thing the Hanged Man never measures, and no honest reading measures it either. What it registers is that your sense of time has been altered by going without — a long enough stretch rearranges how anything feels, until a year of it and the whole rest of a life become indistinguishable from the inside. None of that is a defect in how you think. It is what endurance does to a clock, and you have been judging your entire future from a measurement taken while tired.
4. Let me look closer at the measurement you have been using…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the bundle on his stick is smaller than his head.
2. You asked me about alone and forever. Your hand went to the one who packs light.
3. So forever cannot be made to hold, dear. Not by me.
4. And he carries little, dear, because the far end is unknown.
5. But what I can see is the weight you've been under, dear.
6. That's why you are worn out, dear. I can see that much.
7. Let me look closer at what has been pressing down on you…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

2. Under a question about always, up came the card that knows nothing whatever of always.
3. The Fool holds no forever and cannot be made to hold one; I will not tell you this ends and I will not tell you it does not, because neither sentence would be worth the breath it took. What sits in it instead is that 'forever' is a shape the mind puts around a thing while that thing is still happening, and nobody has ever managed to see out of the middle of something. You are not being asked to believe anything cheerful here. You are being told that the view from where you are standing is not the view.
4. Let me look closer at the view you have been taking for the whole of it…

</details>

# `cards-back-together` — "Will we get back together?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). The US-FRAMED sibling of `reunion`, and that pairing is the whole experiment — reunion casts him as the agent and her as the one waiting; these cast the relationship as a joint thing with two people in it.

🔴 THE FORBIDDEN VERDICT IS ON THE RELATIONSHIP, NOT ON HIM — harder, not softer. A claim about a man can at least be measured against his behaviour; 'it is over' or 'it is not over' is unfalsifiable to her and lands as a ruling on her life from someone who has never met either of them. 🔴 NO ODDS — cards-still-a-chance asks for a number outright and there is none; no percentage, no 'strong chance', no 'likely'. 🔴 NO DIRECTIVES in either direction: move on, let go, fight for him, reach out first are all out. 🔴 Never hand her the outcome ('it is up to you', 'if you had only') — the us-framing makes that easy and it lands as blame.

Finding, and the one that is truest to the us-framing: this question is about a JOINT thing and only one of the two people is in the room. She has been carrying a two-person decision on her own — which is why it will not resolve however long she turns it over.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — his right arm is raised higher than the top of his head.
2. You came asking about getting back together. Your hand went to the man working alone.
3. So this isn't finished, dear. The card won't let me call it that.
4. And on that table, dear, one pair of hands does all the work.
5. But a thing like this takes two, dear, and only you have been working it.
6. That's why nothing moves, however hard you push. You've been the only one pushing.
7. Let me look closer at what has been keeping this a one-person job…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of the thing that only exists if two people build it.
2. Your hand went to the card of what has to be made rather than waited for, and for a question worded the way yours is, that is worth noticing.
3. The Magician holds no picture of the two of you a year from now — what it marks is that the thing you are asking about was never a single event you could watch for. It is two separate decisions that have to meet, and you have been treating it as one outcome to be granted to you. Half of it has always been yours, and you are allowed to know what your own half is well before his ever arrives.
4. Let me look closer at the half of this that has always been yours…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — his tied leg is straight, and the rope is short.
2. Your question was about getting back together. Your hand went to the card that holds the middle.
3. So you've been living in the middle of this, dear. It has no floor.
4. And look at him. Neither down nor up, and quite still.
5. But the middle is a place two people leave together, dear.
6. That's why you keep nearly getting out, dear, and end up back in it.
7. Let me look closer at what has been holding you in the middle…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of what was left standing in the doorway, neither in nor out.
2. You reached for the card of the thing never closed and never resumed, which is the exact place the two of you have been left.
3. The Hanged Man is not in the business of telling you how this ends, and I will not put words in its mouth — what it shows is a thing suspended rather than finished, and two people can be held in a suspension without either of them having chosen it. That is not the same as being over, and it is not the same as being on its way back. What it has cost you is having to live inside it while nobody will name it.
4. Let me look closer at what has been left standing between you…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the stick over his shoulder is long and thin.
2. You asked about the road back together. Your hand went to the one who set out alone.
3. So the road back is still there, dear. Nobody has closed it.
4. And look at him. One road, one traveller, no second pair of boots.
5. But a road back needs two people walking it, dear.
6. That's why rest doesn't fix it, dear. You walked both parts of this.
7. Let me look closer at what has been standing on the road back…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of the road that could still be walked from either end.
2. That is not random; you reached for the card of the beginning, and a beginning is the one thing that cannot be inherited from what came before it.
3. The Fool carries no map of where the two of you end up — what it points at is that whatever opens here would be a different thing wearing a familiar name, not the old one handed back to you intact. You would not be collecting something you left behind. You would be deciding, with the same person and with everything you now know, whether to make something else — and that is a choice you are entitled to make deliberately rather than drift into.
4. Let me look closer at what would actually be beginning…

</details>

# `cards-blocked-retiring` — "Why is my money still blocked this close to retiring?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Money translation: there is no man here, so cut 4 becomes what the block has been DOING, cut 5 becomes the EARNING against the KEEPING, and cut 6 names the near-miss she has lived — the VOC's own sentence. 🔴 NONE of the seven bans below was loosened with the love families; this is the one place where a wrong sentence costs her actual money.

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art.

🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke.

✅ GUARDED as of 2026-08-19: `tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts` now exists and runs against these drafts DIRECTLY. It had to: the drafts setup can only patch a hook the registry already has, so an unwired family is checked by nothing at all and still prints a green tick. All seven bans were fed a deliberate violation and all seven fired.

📊 WHAT THE VOC CHANGED. These hooks have never run, so there is no per-hook data. The nearest real corpus is docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md — 10,514 V1 money concerns. Two themes matter here and they pull in different directions.
**Retirement (554 concerns, the highest earner in the bucket at $15,146/1k):** the emotion is not panic, it is quiet arithmetic dread. 'I'm worried that I'll outlive my few savings.' 'Prices are going up but my pension is not.' 'I thought I would be set by now.'
**The invisible block (1,208 concerns) — and its instruction is explicit: 'The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty.'** 'I keep trying to get ahead and no matter what I do nothing seems to work out, it's like something is holding me back.' 'I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish.' 'Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't.'
So these reads are built on the NEAR-MISS, not on being short of money. That also satisfies ban 7 for free — nothing here presumes she is broke, only that she has got close and watched it go.

Tendency: her ability was never the problem, and something stands between the EARNING and the KEEPING. Never name what or who, never a sum, a date or a source, never suggest anything be done with money.

# `cards-cant-stop` — "Why can't I stop thinking about him?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO DIRECTIVE — 'let him go', 'time to move on', 'try to forget him' are what she has been told by everyone already, and they are not a reading. 🔴 No promise about him and 🔴 no pathologising: obsessed, unhealthy, 'you are stuck' are all banned. 🔴 No timeframe.

Finding, kept distinct from its two siblings: the word 'stop'. She has framed her own mind as a machine she is failing to switch off, and has been trying to win by force. Minds do not take orders — and the trying is costing more than the thinking.

3. So you've been trying to stop by force, dear. That's the tiring bit.
4. And his hands are busy, dear, with no off switch anywhere on him.
5. But a mind doesn't take orders like that, dear. Not one.
6. That's why the trying costs you more than the thinking does, dear.
7. Let me look closer at what has kept you fighting your own head…

# `cards-cheating` — "Is he cheating on you?"

⛔ The decode-him DECK was retired 2026-08-19 (operator call) — its strip art was the fb-palm thumb image and no ad ever pointed at it. Its block has been removed from this draft; the arcana and return-mhf decks are unaffected.

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why. 🔴 THIS FAMILY WAS DELIBERATELY NOT LOOSENED: cut 3 still answers her FEAR (am I short of sense, or short of facts) and never rules on him in either direction. The art line and the bridge are unchanged.

Lives on ALL FOUR decks — three card-sets: Magician/Hanged Man/Fool (return-mhf + arcana-mfh, one copy, verb swapped), Emperor/Empress/Fool (arcana-eef), Sun/Moon/Tower (decode-him). The arcana-eef Fool reuses the MHF Fool copy verb-swapped.

🔴 THE FLAGGED WORD IS AVOIDED IN EVERY BEAT — 'cheat' and its forms appear nowhere, which is what the live copy on three of the four decks already does (decode-him has one slip today; this rewrite removes it). The ad headline carries the word; the read does not. The echo still lands because the gate keys on 'you'.

🔴 NEVER SAY HE HAS OR HAS NOT. TAROT_HOOK_TENDENCY: 'that the unease she feels is real information about her situation, not paranoia to apologize for. NEVER say he is cheating or that he is faithful as fact.' So what gets certified is her UNEASE — a signal she has been apologising for — and what gets named is that no answer was ever put beside it.

This hook is the INCUMBENT the whole fidelity family is tested against: tests/tarot-fidelity-copy.test.ts requires those four landers to share no 6-word run with it on any deck. Its own finding is kept distinct from all four — unease as a real signal, where cards-someone-else takes the GAP, cards-loyal the PORTION, cards-talking-someone the ATTENTION, cards-faithful the REST.

🔴 decode-him takes no picture line — its strip is the fb-PALM thumb art and there is no revealStrip, so there is no card for her to look at. Beat 1 uses the card-name form, and all three are written fresh (all four hooks on that deck currently share one).

# `cards-come-back` — "Will he come back?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO FORECAST IN EITHER DIRECTION — 'he will come back' and 'he has moved on' are both things no card knows, and the second is the one that sounds like honesty. 🔴 Never blame her for waiting: naive, too trusting, foolish, clinging, desperate and 'wasted your time' are all banned outright. 🔴 No timeframe, no guarantee.

⚠️ SHARES ITS HEADLINE WITH cards-return ON PURPOSE — the two are a deliberate A/B on the same ad question, so this read has to be genuinely its own or the comparison is dead. cards-return's finding is that he is not short of a way back and the pull is not only hers; this one takes the DOOR SHE HAS BEEN HOLDING — the waiting is a job she has been doing alone with no one to relieve her.

4. And it's one person doing all the holding, dear. Look at him.

# `cards-deceived` — "Am I being deceived?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO VERDICT IN EITHER DIRECTION. These three name an untruth outright, so both answers are live harms: 'he lied to you' is an accusation against a real man on no evidence, and 'he is telling you the truth' is a reassurance the funnel has no standing to give — and on this audience the second is the more dangerous of the two. 🔴 Never blame her judgment: naive, gullible, too trusting, 'should have known' are all out. 🔴 No timeframe.

Finding: 'deceived' is a heavier word than 'lied to' — it means a whole picture, not one sentence. She is asking whether the shape of the thing is what she thinks. ⚠️ Its beat 2 could not restate her headline: 'you are being deceived' is itself a banned phrase, so the echo is built as 'you asked about being deceived'.

# `cards-end-up-alone` — "Why do I keep ending up alone?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO KIND DIAGNOSIS — 'you love too much', 'you are too strong', 'your standards intimidate them' are the internet's stock answers and every one is a verdict wearing a compliment. 🔴 NO CAUSE asserted, 🔴 no prerequisite framing ('until you love yourself'), 🔴 no ruling that she has given up, and 🔴 no claiming access to her interior — 'deep down you', 'part of you knows', 'what you really want' are all banned.

Finding: the question assumes a pattern with a cause, and the honest answer is that a mind will always find one. She has been the only suspect in her own case for years and nobody has ever told her it was not her.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — his cloak falls open down one side.
2. You asked why you keep ending up alone. Your hand went to the man who looks for reasons.
3. So there's no reason to find here, dear. You've hunted for one for years.
4. And a mind like his can build a reason out of anything, dear.
5. But a reason you built yourself isn't a finding, dear.
6. That's why every answer you land on has your own name in it.
7. Let me look closer at what keeps landing in the same place…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of the maker, which is a very different thing from the made.
2. Your hand went to the card about authorship, and authorship is exactly what your question has been quietly assuming.
3. There is no reason here for me to give you, and whatever I produced would be something I had made up on the spot to fit the shape of your question — that is the honest answer, and it is worth more than a kind invention. Your question contains a hidden claim: that there is a single cause, that it is located in you, and that a stranger could read it off a card. The Magician is the card of what a person actually authors, and what anybody authors is their own conduct — never the whole outcome, which needed another person to show up and keep showing up. You have been holding yourself accountable for a result that was never yours alone to produce.
4. Let me look closer at what you have been holding yourself responsible for…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — his knee makes a sharp corner against the beam.
2. You asked why you keep ending up alone. Your hand went to the card that hangs a why upside down.
3. So nothing falls out when you turn this over, dear. I've watched you try.
4. And he hangs there turned over, dear, and still no answer drops.
5. But some things just happen, dear. There's no reason underneath them.
6. That's why you've been the only suspect in your own case, dear.
7. Let me look closer at what has been standing in for an answer…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of the picture that has been hanging upside down.
2. Yours was the card of the inverted view, and this question has been sitting inverted in you for a long while.
3. The word doing the work in what you asked is 'keep', and it is the one I want to turn the right way up. Separate endings, each with its own reasons and its own other person, get gathered up by the mind and read backwards as a single pattern with a single culprit — and once that is done, the culprit is always the one person present at all of them, which is you. That is not evidence, dear. That is arithmetic performed while tired. The Hanged Man does not tell me why any of them ended, and neither will I, because the truthful answer is that they are not one thing and were never one thing.
4. Let me look closer at the pattern you have been reading into it…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the dog's mouth is open, and it's right at his heel.
2. You asked why you keep ending up alone. Your hand went to the one who keeps setting out.
3. So you keep setting out again, dear. That is the part worth seeing.
4. And he takes the same step at the same edge, dear, and goes anyway.
5. But this card counts no failures, dear. It keeps no tally at all.
6. That's why the tally hurts, dear. Nobody else was keeping one.
7. Let me look closer at what keeps meeting you at the same edge…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card carrying no verdict on anybody, least of all the one holding it.
2. You came asking to be told what is wrong with you, and up came the card that has never once made that finding.
3. There is nothing in this card that says you are the reason, and there is nothing in it that says you are not — I will not rule on you in either direction, because it is not a ruling anybody is entitled to make and you would carry it either way. What the Fool sets down instead is that your question was built as a case against yourself, and then handed to me to confirm. I am not going to confirm it. Endings happen for reasons that mostly live in other people and in circumstances, and a woman is not a defect for having been present at hers.
4. Let me look closer at the case you have been building against yourself…

</details>

# `cards-energy-how-long` — "How long has my energy been working against my money?"

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art. Written 2026-08-19 straight to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework, so bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain (so / and / but / that's why). Money translation: cut 3 answers her fear about HERSELF ("it was never you"), cut 4 says where the thing in the way actually sits, cut 5 is EARNING against KEEPING, cut 6 is the NEAR-MISS.

🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel, and NONE was loosened with the love families. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration, no deserving. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke.

📊 Built on the NEAR-MISS, not on poverty — docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md §3 says so outright ('The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty'): "I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish", "Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't". That clears ban 7 for free.

✅ GUARDED: tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts now exists and runs against these drafts directly (the drafts setup can only patch hooks that are already in the registry, so an unwired family would otherwise be checked by nothing at all).

Tendency: her energy has not been working against her at all — refuse the premise gently — and whatever settled on this settled long ago and was never named. 🔴 NEVER give a length of time and never date it to an event in her life.

4. And both his hands are in the present, dear. Nothing of his is ahead.
5. But now you're asking about the rest of your life, dear.
6. That's why the word ever slipped in, dear. It came without you noticing.
7. Let me look closer at what has stretched this out so far…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of what a person keeps aiming their life at.
2. Your hand found the card of sustained intention, which is a fair description of what you have been doing here.
3. The Magician gives me nothing I could promise you — it says the time you have gone on pointing at this was time you genuinely meant, and whatever he does with what is left of it, the steadiness in you was never the part that went to waste.
4. Let me look closer at where all that steadiness has actually been going…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — you can see pale sky right through the gap under him.
2. You asked if he will ever come back to you. Your hand went to the card that keeps a thing hanging.
3. So nothing has ended, dear, and the card gives no end date.
4. And he's held there, dear, with pale sky showing right through the gap.
5. But ever isn't about him any more, dear. It's about you.
6. That's why the question got heavier, dear. You changed what you were asking.
7. Let me look closer at what has been holding you in place…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of the long wait, of time spent living inside a question.
2. You reached for the card of suspension, and it is your own you have been hanging in, not his.
3. The Hanged Man declines the ever in your question — it says you have been asked to live in an unanswered thing far longer than anyone should be asked to, and the tiredness underneath your asking is not weakness, it is the honest weight of having waited without ever being told anything.
4. Let me look closer at what the waiting has been asking of you…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the bundle is red and tied with a small knot.
2. You asked if he will ever come back to you. Your hand went to the one who packs and goes.
3. So you're still able to move, dear. That's what this card shows me.
4. And nothing weighs him down, dear. He's packed and ready to go.
5. But you've kept your own bag packed for a long time, dear.
6. That's why standing still costs you so much, dear. You were made to move.
7. Let me look closer at what has been keeping you standing still…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of the page that has stayed blank.
2. Your hand went to the card of what has never been written on, which is where this has sat for a long while now.
3. The Fool holds out no guarantee of him — it says anything opening here again would have to start as a new thing rather than resume as the old one, and the man you have been keeping the place set for may not be the one who could walk into it; knowing that now protects you far better than hoping around it.
4. Let me look closer at who it is you have been keeping that place for…

</details>

# `cards-faithful` — "Is he being faithful to me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why. 🔴 NOT LOOSENED: no verdict on a third person in EITHER direction, so cut 3 answers her FEAR (was something real taken from me, or am I making it up) and never rules on him. Each hook keeps its own finding — GAP / PORTION / ATTENTION / REST — which its guard file pins in beat 3.

Face-down only (return-mhf) — the family is pinned off the face-up decks.

🔴 THE FLAGGED WORD (cheat / cheating / affair / infidelity) MAY NOT APPEAR IN ANY BEAT. 🔴 And the paranoia frame is banned in BOTH directions — no 'paranoid', 'imagining it', 'overthinking', 'insecure', 'crazy'. Calling her it is the injury; using it to reassure her plants it. That removes the absolution form used everywhere else on the funnel, so each of these names the accusation in other words.

🔴 Never assert a third person and never deny one. Never instruct her to check a phone, gather proof, or confront him — the most available wrong answer to these questions anywhere on the internet. Never excuse him, never land it as her fault. Four separate findings, or the family is one lander wearing four headlines.

Finding: it refuses the summary judgment and routes to HER NOT RESTING. She is not asking for a verdict — she is asking for permission to put it down, and no card can hand her the guarantee that would let her.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — he stands square behind his table and doesn't lean.
2. You asked if he's being faithful to you. Your hand went to the steadiest man in this deck.
3. So you haven't had a moment's rest from this, dear. Not one.
4. And look at him. He stands square, and you have not been able to.
5. But no card gives you a guarantee, dear. This steady one won't either.
6. That's why you keep asking, dear. Asking is the only thing that quiets it.
7. Let me look closer at what keeps you from setting this down…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of the thing tested by doing rather than by declaring.
2. The card that came to your hand is the one about practice, and practice is a longer question than the one you asked.
3. No card issues a character reference, and I would be overstepping badly if I read one out of this — I will not vouch for him and I will not convict him, because a summary judgment on a whole person is not mine to hand down and it is not the Magician's either. What sits in front of me is nearer and more useful: you asked because you have not been able to put the question down. Not being able to rest is a fact about your life, whatever the answer turns out to be, and it is the fact I can actually speak to.
4. Let me look closer at why you have not been able to put it down…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — the rope is on his right ankle, and it holds.
2. You asked if he's being faithful to you. Your hand went to the card that holds its breath.
3. So the question has stayed up, dear. Nothing has let it down.
4. And you've been holding it up on your own, dear. All of it.
5. But a thing held that long stops feeling like a question, dear.
6. That's why you're worn out, dear. You never once got to rest.
7. Let me look closer at what won't let this settle…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of the verdict that never arrives.
2. Yours was the card of the thing left pending, and pending is where this question has lived in you.
3. The Hanged Man declines the reassurance you came for, and declines its reverse with exactly the same firmness; that refusal is the honest part of this rather than a dodge. A word like the one in your question describes a whole person across a whole stretch of time, and nobody standing outside a life gets to certify such a thing. What I can see is that you have been left hanging in the asking, and being held there drains something out of you that neither a clear yes nor a clear no ever would.
4. Let me look closer at what the suspension has taken out of you…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — his stick rests on his shoulder, and the bundle swings behind.
2. You asked if he's being faithful to you. Your hand went to the one with no fixed ground.
3. So you've had no firm footing here, dear. Look at his feet.
4. And look — rock on one side of him, and air on the other.
5. But you've been standing on that all the same, dear.
6. That's why you can't put this down. There's been nothing to put it on.
7. Let me look closer at what has been keeping this off solid ground…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of the thing that is chosen again each day rather than settled once.
2. You found the card of the fresh step under a question that wanted a permanent answer, and that mismatch is worth sitting with.
3. The Fool declines to stamp anybody and I decline alongside it — what it draws attention to is that the quality you are asking about is not a fixed property somebody either has or lacks. It is chosen, repeatedly, by people who could choose otherwise. That is why no reading can hand you a guarantee, and why the reassurance you have been hoping for would have been worth very little even if I had given it to you. What you actually want is to be able to trust your own footing again.
4. Let me look closer at the footing you have been missing…

</details>

# `cards-feel-about-me` — "How does he really feel about me?"

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework, after the interior ban was loosened. The read may now ANSWER: the card carries the claim about him. 🔴 THE DENIAL IS STILL BANNED — 'he does not love you' is the one direction she has no use for. 🔴 NO DIAGNOSIS: avoidant, emotionally unavailable, commitment-phobic, narcissist. 🔴 Never grade her (naive, foolish, gave too much). 🔴 No dates. The seven cuts run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why / let me look closer — and every cut must add information the last one did not carry.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the four things on the table are all within his reach.
2. You asked how he really feels about you. Your hand went to the card of a man who chooses.
3. So he feels it, dear. The card is plain with me about that.
4. And he has chosen how much of it reaches you. That choosing is done on purpose.
5. But choosing what to show you is not the same as feeling less.
6. That's why he can seem near one day, dear, and measured the next.
7. Let me look closer at what sits behind the part he keeps back…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of the hands rather than the heart.
2. You asked what he feels, and drew the card of what actually gets done about it.
3. I want to say something about your question before I say anything at all about the card. You have come to a woman you have never met to find out how a man who knows you feels about you. I am not saying that to shame you, dear — I am saying it because it is information, and it is the one piece nobody ever puts in front of you. The Magician does not report anybody's heart to me, and I would not pass it on if it did. It deals only in what is done, and how consistently it is done. Somewhere in what has been done, or has not been, sits the reason you had to come and ask me instead of simply knowing. You are allowed to want that gap named, dear, rather than quietly working around it for another year.
4. Let me look closer at why you had to come and ask…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — his face is the lowest thing on the card.
2. You asked how he really feels about you. Your hand went to the card of a man seen upside down.
3. So he does feel it, dear. You've been picking that up correctly.
4. And there is more of it in him than that angle has let you see.
5. But hanging there, he cannot turn himself the right way up.
6. That's why you keep getting halves of him, dear, and never the whole.
7. Let me look closer at what has been holding him in that position…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of the answer that will not settle.
2. It was the card of what refuses to come to rest that your hand found, and that is where this has been living.
3. There is one word carrying your entire question, dear, and it is 'really'. It means some answer has already been handed to you and it did not hold — it slid off, or it stopped matching what you could see — and here you are, asking a second time, of a stranger. What he feels I cannot reach and would not guess at. What the Hanged Man says is that an answer which has to be asked for twice has not done its work, and that having to ask again reflects nothing whatever about you. It reflects on what you were told the first time.
4. Let me look closer at the answer that would not hold…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the dog is barking and he hasn't turned round.
2. You asked how he really feels about you. Your hand went to the card of a man mid-step.
3. So he feels it, dear. What you picked up from him was there.
4. And this card walks on without once looking back at what it left.
5. But walking on is a habit in him. It is not a ruling on you.
6. That's why the warmth reached you, dear, and the account never did.
7. Let me look closer at what keeps him from stopping long enough…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of the one standing at the start with nothing settled.
2. You came for a report on somebody's heart and drew the card that has never once carried a guarantee.
3. This card carries no confession, dear, and dressing one up for you would lighten tonight at the expense of every night after it. But I do not believe a report was ever really what you came for. Underneath 'how does he feel' there is nearly always a second question — whether to keep going, and how much more of yourself to put in — and that one is yours rather than his, and it does not require his answer first. The Fool will not tell you what is in him. It is quite firm, though, that you are allowed to decide what you need before you ever find out.
4. Let me look closer at the question underneath the question…

</details>

# `cards-feels` — "How does he really feel about you?"

⛔ The decode-him DECK was retired 2026-08-19 (operator call) — its strip art was the fb-palm thumb image and no ad ever pointed at it. Its block has been removed from this draft; the arcana and return-mhf decks are unaffected.

Card-sets differ by deck, so the copy does too: Magician/Hanged Man/Fool (return-mhf + arcana-mfh), Sun/Moon/Tower (decode-him), Emperor/Empress/Fool (arcana-eef). The two Magician decks share one set, verb swapped. 🔴 decode-him's beat 1s are written fresh rather than kept: all four hooks on that deck currently open with the SAME line per card (no guard file catches it — see the duplication note in the checklist).

🔴 BEAT 4 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 — it must name an OBSTRUCTION, not an absence. Act 1 of the trilogy sells an Energy Clearing Ritual that removes "the shadow that's been blocking your path"; improve-v1/08-clearing-theme-coherence.md found that clearing is SPRUNG at the pitch rather than seeded. This is where it gets seeded, and the object goes between HER and what she wants — the love bucket frames every block as an impersonal thing in her path precisely so removing it blames nobody. "What he never said" cannot be cleared; "what's sitting between you and the words" can.

🔴 BEAT 3 CORRECTED 2026-08-19. tests/tarot-real-feelings-copy.test.ts fired — a deliberate tripwire on this hook, because cards-feels is the CONTROL for two comparisons. The first rewrite dropped the old copy's "leans toward" hedge to reach grade 5, which turned a tendency into a flat verdict on his heart ("He does feel it, dear") — exactly what this hook's TAROT_HOOK_TENDENCY bans. Plain English did not require it: "You haven't been reading coldness, dear. You've been reading a hold." says the same thing at the same grade, about HER reading rather than his interior. Rule applied: no sentence may take "he" as the subject of a feeling verb stated as fact; negations ("doesn't mean he's cold") and card-tendency forms ("it reads as") are fine.

3. So the warmth you felt was not made up, dear.
4. A man like this doesn't warm to someone by accident.
5. What he hasn't done is say it out loud.
6. That gap is real, dear. You've been reading it right.
7. Let me look closer at what's sitting between you and the words…

# `cards-feels-off` — "Something feels off — is my intuition right?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NEVER CONVICT HIM and 🔴 never dismiss what she noticed — both doors are shut. 🔴🔴 AND NEVER NAME CONTENTS: no other woman, no affair, no money, no secret family, no messages from anyone. Naming a content is the single most harmful thing this pair could do, because she will go looking for it. 🔴 No surveillance and no 'confront him'.

⚠️ THIS HOOK SUBMITS HER OWN JUDGEMENT FOR A VERDICT, which is a trap: 'yes, you are right' convicts him through her, and 'no, you are wrong' teaches her to distrust the one instrument she has. So the question is SPLIT rather than answered — the read certifies that she registered something real, and leaves what it means entirely open. (The word 'intuition' is four syllables and cannot appear at grade 5; the echo lands on 'feels', 'off' and 'right' instead.)

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the smallest thing on his table is the coin.
2. You asked if something feels off, and if you're right. Your hand went to the man who notices detail.
3. So you picked up something small, dear. That's real.
4. And the coin is easy to miss, dear, and it's still there.
5. But noticing is one job, dear. Reading it is another.
6. That's why the first part is done, dear, and the second is still open.
7. Let me look closer at what your eye caught on…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of the mind that registers a thing long before it can explain it.
2. You asked whether your sense of this is sound, and drew the card of the working intelligence.
3. I want to be careful with you here, because there are two questions inside that one and they do not have the same answer. Did you notice something — yes. The Magician is emphatic that this is a faculty working rather than a nerve misfiring. What the noticed thing MEANS is the part I will not hand you, dear, and I would be lying if I dressed a guess up as a reading. Nor will I tell you that intuition is never wrong — a flattering sentence which has cost women a great deal, because it quietly turns every fear into a finding. The noticing is real. What it points to is still open.
4. Let me look closer at what it was you actually noticed…

3. So your eye is telling you this sits wrong, dear. It's working.
4. And not one line on that card is level, dear. You saw it at once.
5. But what your eye has found is a separate question, dear.

2. It was the card of the mismatch your hand found, and a mismatch is very often what 'off' turns out to be made of.
3. A feeling of wrongness is usually not mystical, dear, whatever you have been told — most often it is the ordinary result of being given an account that does not match what you can see, and a mind doing its job by flagging the difference. Which of the two is the false one is beyond what this card knows. But I am not going to do the more common thing either and suggest you have produced all of this out of insecurity. Being talked out of your own observations is how a person ends up unable to trust herself at all. Something did not line up. That is a finding, and it stands whatever turns out to explain it.
4. Let me look closer at what stopped lining up…

</details>

3. So you looked, dear. That's a real difference between you and him.
4. And he doesn't look, dear, and the edge is right behind his heel.
5. But I won't rule on what your looking found, dear.
6. That's why looking early counts, dear, whatever it turns out to mean.

2. You came to have your sense checked, and up came the card of the one who must move without a verdict in hand.
3. No card is going to confirm this for you, dear, and it would be an unkindness to pretend one had. The Fool is honest about that much: you are standing where a person stands before they know. What I will not let pass is the notion that you must earn the right to a straight answer by first assembling proof. You do not. Somewhere along the way, asking plainly and accusing got collapsed into the same act for you, and they were never the same act. The unease you arrived carrying is not evidence against him — I will not let it be used that way. It is also not nothing, and you were not wrong to bring it here.
4. Let me look closer at what you have been made to prove before you could ask…

</details>

Both poles stay banned — never "you are his forever" (a promise), never "you are only his now" (a dismissal).

VOC: this family is 5 days old and has 14 quotes across all five hooks, so voc-by-hook could not do the job. Read 769 real V1 concerns on the married-man/other-woman theme instead (new scripts/voc-by-theme.mjs). What she types: "he calls me his wife but there is no actions", "I'm tired of not being chosen", "I will no longer be a secret in his life", "he presented as though his relationship with his wife was over". She has felt forever and never once been TOLD it. So the payoff certifies the feeling and the gap names the silence — she has been reading a silence as a verdict already passed on her.

So the honest answer is not one of your two, dear. It is a question back: has anything actually been built here, or has it only been felt? It is the building that has gone missing.

What the Hanged Man shows instead is far more ordinary and far more fixable: you are in an arrangement that has never been named, and the not-naming is the thing you are actually feeling. But hear what I am not saying — undecided is not the same as doomed, and it is not the same as promised, and the wait itself is not proof of either. There is a difference between a road with no map and a road with no destination, and you are allowed to ask which of them you are walking.
4. Let me look closer at the road you have actually been walking…

</details>

# `cards-ghosted` — "Why did he ghost me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NEVER SUPPLY HIS REASON — not scared, not overwhelmed, not 'he couldn't cope', not 'he left because', not 'the reason he'. The reason lives in a man who did not give it, and inventing one hands her something false to act on. 🔴 Never a verdict on his person, 🔴 never invent a third person, and 🔴 note that 'it was never about you' and 'he was protecting you' are banned too — they are motive claims wearing reassurance. 🔴 No tactic in either direction: no reaching out, no one last message, no blocking, no no-contact. 🔴 No promise of closure.

Finding: she has been handed a silence and asked to author its meaning. The read refuses to author it for her and names the work she has been doing instead.

# `cards-given-up` — "Have I given up on love without realizing it?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO KIND DIAGNOSIS — 'you love too much', 'you are too strong', 'your standards intimidate them' are the internet's stock answers and every one is a verdict wearing a compliment. 🔴 NO CAUSE asserted, 🔴 no prerequisite framing ('until you love yourself'), 🔴 no ruling that she has given up, and 🔴 no claiming access to her interior — 'deep down you', 'part of you knows', 'what you really want' are all banned.

🔴 THE HEADLINE PREMISE MAY NOT BE ASSERTED — 'you have given up' is explicitly the banned direction, and so is 'without realising it, you…'. Both answers are a stranger ruling on her interior. So the read declines the premise and points at the one thing that is checkable from here: she is on this page, asking.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — he looks out past you, not quite at you.
2. You asked about giving up on love without knowing it. Your hand went to the man still at work.
3. So I won't rule on your heart from outside it, dear. Nobody can.
4. And here you are, asking, dear. A woman who had quit wouldn't be.
5. But the question got in somehow, dear. Something put it there.
6. That's why you ask this at night, dear, and the day gives no answer.
7. Let me look closer at what has been sitting on top of your hope…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of the hands that have not stopped, whatever the person attached to them believes.
2. You reached for the card of ongoing work at the exact moment you were wondering whether you had put the work down.
3. What you have or have not given up on is not mine to announce, dear, and no card gets to rule on the inside of a person — you read your own interior better than anyone alive, and certainly better than this deck. What I can say is that you are here, at whatever hour this is, asking. That is not the conduct of someone who has closed the matter; a closed matter does not get taken out and examined. Whether the hope has changed shape is a real question and yours alone to answer, but the Magician does not find a woman who has stopped. It finds one who is worried she might have, which is nearly the opposite.
4. Let me look closer at what you are afraid you have let go of…

**NEW — what she would read**

2. You asked about giving up on love without knowing it. Your hand went to the card that only looks still.
3. So still isn't the same as finished, dear. Look at his eyes.
4. And he's wide awake up there, dear, with nothing moving at all.
5. But rest and quitting look alike from outside, dear.
6. That's why you've read your own rest as a verdict, dear.
7. Let me look closer at what has been keeping you this still…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

2. It was the card of the held moment that came to your hand, and holding is precisely what your question is about.
3. Protecting yourself is not the same as giving up, though from the outside — and after enough years — the two can look identical, which is what has you asking. Somewhere you began expecting less out loud so that less would hurt less. That is not surrender, dear, it is a sensible thing a person does after being disappointed enough times, and it is reversible in a way that giving up is not. Neither of those is mine to score, and naming which one this is would be a judgement I have no standing whatever to make. The Hanged Man's business is what the holding has cost, never a verdict on the woman doing the holding.
4. Let me look closer at what the guarding has been costing you…

3. So you're mid-step, dear. You have not sat down.
4. And nobody steps off a cliff having quit, dear. Look at his foot.
5. But this card can't say what's inside you, dear. No card can.

3. Let me take 'without realizing it' out of your hands, dear, because that is the phrase doing the damage. It supposes something has happened inside you that you cannot see and a stranger can — and that is not true of me, or of this card, or of anyone who will ever tell you otherwise. You would know. It might be buried under a great deal of self-protection and a fair amount of tiredness, but it would be yours to find, not mine to announce. The Fool carries no closed doors. What it carries is that the wanting is still in you, or this question would never have been worth typing at one in the morning.
4. Let me look closer at what is still in there under the guarding…

</details>

# `cards-her-shadow` — "Am I living in her shadow?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 now run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS instead of certifying her. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every family ban below still stands; this family was NOT loosened.

⚠️ "her" may be an EX or a woman who has DIED, and the headline does not say. 🔴 NEVER presume which. 🔴🔴 MEDIUMSHIP BAN (this family runs the decode-him frame, which carries none of it): never that she is at peace, watching, or would want anything. 🔴 NEVER disparage or characterise her, never make her a rival, never rank the two women. And the tightrope: never confirm the comparison as fact, never tell her she is imagining it.

VOC (769 V1 concerns, married-man/other-woman theme) settles why that tightrope is real: the other woman is very often NOT a rival — she is dead, ill, dependent, or entirely legitimate. "He lost his wife of 57 years a short while ago." "His wife is in bad health... I certainly would not hurt his wife." "I'm not a marriage breaker." Handing this reader an enemy would be harm dressed as comfort.

So the split, exactly as `hidden-intuition` splits its question: certify HER FEELING (she has felt something in the room) and refuse to rule on the woman at all. The finding does the rest — a shadow is not a person, it is what falls when something blocks the light. Which makes bubble 7 the easiest object on the funnel: the thing blocking the light IS the block Act 1 clears.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the number one sits at the very top of his card.
2. You asked if you're living in her shadow. Your hand went to the man who deals in solid things.
3. So there is something in that room with you, dear. The card gives you that.
4. And a shadow isn't a person, dear. It's what falls when the light is blocked.
5. But nobody has ever set out the terms, dear. You've been guessing at them.
6. That's why a good day with him can still end with you comparing, dear.

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of the thing itself, as against the shape it throws.
2. You asked about a shadow, and drew the card that deals only in what is solid and actually made.
3. Consider what a shadow really is, dear, because your own word is doing more work than you realise. A shadow is not a person. It is what falls when something stands between you and the light. What she was, or is, I shall stay out of entirely — inventing a woman for you to be set against is beneath what you came here for. What the Magician will say is this. You are being measured against something you have never been shown — not a person, but a version of one. Versions get edited, dear. By memory, which is a poor and flattering archivist. By grief. Nobody alive can be compared against an edited version and come out level, and you have been quietly failing that comparison without ever being shown its terms.
4. Let me look closer at the comparison nobody has ever shown you…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — his hands are tucked behind his back, out of sight.
2. You asked if you're living in her shadow. Your hand went to the man hung upside down.
3. So no measure was ever set out for you, dear. His hands are behind his back.
4. And the light in this comes from him, dear. He decides what gets shown.
5. But you can't answer a question that was never asked out loud, dear.
6. That's why you go looking for signs of her, dear, and you keep finding them.
7. Let me look closer at what's casting this over you…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card that asks who is holding the lamp.
2. Your hand went to the card of the reversal, and the reversal here is not about her at all.
3. You have asked whether you are in her shadow, and every part of your attention is pointed at her. Turn it round, dear. A comparison does not make itself. Somebody has to hold it up, and that is the question worth your evening. Sometimes it is him, in small ways he may not even hear himself doing. Sometimes — and I say this with no unkindness — it is her name doing work inside your own head, because no one has ever sat you down and told you plainly that you are not being weighed. You have no way of knowing which of those it is, dear, and that is not a failure of your perception.
4. Let me look closer at who has been holding this up…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — mountains behind him, and one step left before the drop.
2. You asked if you're living in her shadow. Your hand went to the one who came in late.
3. So you came in after her, dear. Coming later doesn't put you under anyone.
4. And look at him, dear. He walks in with nothing settled and no past to carry.
5. But the order still gets treated like a ranking, dear. No one has said otherwise.
6. That's why the good days still leave you checking, dear. You've had no word to hold.
7. Let me look closer at what stands between you and being seen plainly…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of the one who arrives after the story has begun.
2. You came asking where you stand beside another woman, and drew the card of the one who walked in with nothing settled.
3. You came into this after her. That is a fact about the order of events, dear — no verdict on your worth lives anywhere inside it. Now, I will not do the thing you may have half hoped for. I will not weigh you against her, I will not suggest she was less than you have been told, and I will not hand you an enemy to feel taller beside. Whoever she is or was, she is not your opponent. What the Fool holds is simpler and it is entirely yours. You have the right to be looked at as yourself rather than in relation to somebody else — to be a person in this life rather than the chapter that came afterwards.
4. Let me look closer at what it would mean to be seen as yourself…

</details>

# `cards-hiding-something` — "Is he hiding something from me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NEVER CONVICT HIM and 🔴 never dismiss what she noticed — both doors are shut. 🔴🔴 AND NEVER NAME CONTENTS: no other woman, no affair, no money, no secret family, no messages from anyone. Naming a content is the single most harmful thing this pair could do, because she will go looking for it. 🔴 No surveillance and no 'confront him'.

Finding: an omission is not a lie, and she has been asked to prove a negative — which cannot be done, which is why this has gone round and round. What can be certified is the noticing.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — he stands where you can see him, and the table hides the rest.
2. You asked about him hiding something. Your hand went to the man who decides what's on show.
3. So you've been shown a front, dear. Fronts are chosen, and that's real.
4. And the table covers him from the waist down, dear.
5. But a thing left out isn't a lie told, dear. Those are different.
6. That's why you have no name for it, dear, and still feel it there.
7. Let me look closer at what has been standing in the covered part…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of the one who decides what is shown and what is kept back.
2. You asked whether something is being kept from you, and up came the card of deliberate arrangement.
3. Whether something is genuinely being kept from you is not a thing this card will settle, dear, and I would not invent its contents to fill the silence — a stranger with a deck telling a woman what a man conceals is making it up. What the Magician marks is management: a hand deciding, in the moment, how much of a picture to hand across. Everyone alive does a little of that. What you are describing is not the everyday amount, or the question would never have been worth typing. The card convicts him of nothing and neither will I. It says the edge you keep meeting is a made one — and meeting an edge is not the same as imagining one.
4. Let me look closer at where the picture keeps stopping…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — his hands are behind him where the picture stops.
2. You asked about him hiding something. Your hand went to the card with a back you can't see.
3. So there's a back to this you can't reach, dear. You noticed that.
4. And the working half of him is out of the frame, dear.
5. But you've been asked to prove a thing isn't there, dear.
6. That's why it goes round and round, dear. Nobody can prove that.
7. Let me look closer at what keeps this half out of frame…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of what is held just out of view.
2. It was the card of the withheld thing that came to your hand, and withholding is precisely what you came to ask about.
3. What he keeps is not something this card can show me, dear, and anyone who claims otherwise is performing — nor am I going to send you looking for it. Going through a man's phone has never once produced peace; it produces a worse version of the same night. The Hanged Man turns the thing over instead. You have been doing the work of guessing, and guessing is labour — hours of you, spent assembling a picture you were never handed whole. The needing to do that is not imaginary, whatever sits behind it. Being made to build the thing yourself is a cost you have already paid in full.
4. Let me look closer at what the guessing has been costing you…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the bundle on his stick is closed and tied shut.
2. You asked about him hiding something. Your hand went to the one who carries a closed bag.
3. So there's a closed bag here, dear. That much is real.
4. And it's tied shut, dear, and I won't tell you what's inside.
5. But no honest reader names what's in a shut bag, dear.
6. That's why you circle it, dear. You saw the bag, and it stayed shut.
7. Let me look closer at what has been keeping that bag shut…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of the man who has not sat down and worked out what he thinks.
2. Your hand went to the card of the unexamined thing, and that is a genuinely different animal from the concealed one.
3. Here the Fool asks me to slow down before either of us calls this a secret. Not everything withheld was ever decided on; some of it has simply never been faced by the man carrying it, and a thing he has not admitted to himself cannot be told to you — which lands on your side identically either way, as a gap. I will not rule on which of the two this is, dear, because from the outside they are indistinguishable. What the card will not do is put the gap in your imagination. The gap is there. What it is made of is the open question, and it is a fair one to have.
4. Let me look closer at the shape of the gap…

</details>

# `cards-his-children` — "Why do his children come before me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 now run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS instead of certifying her. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every family ban below still stands; this family was NOT loosened.

🔴🔴 THE SHARPEST LANDER ON THE FUNNEL. It asks a card to comment on a man's CHILDREN — real third parties, possibly minors, who cannot consent to being read. NEVER rank her against them in either direction, never characterise them, never grade him as a father. The test pins BOTH halves of the payout: beat 3 must say plainly that children DO have a first call (`first call`), AND must offer a DEFINED place rather than a higher one (`place of your own` / `where you sit` / `appear on one` / `planned for you`).

VOC (192 V1 concerns on the his-children theme): the injury is NOT rank. Not one woman asked to outrank a child. What they typed: "his daughter is getting married in July and I'm not invited... I've been Keith's partner for nine years", "he had a whole life without me", "I feel rejected from the family", "he lets them disrespect me". She is not on the list at all — in either direction. So the read says the true thing first (first call is real), then names the blur that actually hurts: first call was never the same as only call.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — a gold cup and a coin marked with a star, set out in order.
2. You asked why his children come before you. Your hand went to the man who plans.
3. So his children do have a first call, dear. I won't dress that up for you.
4. And first call was never the only call, dear. Look how much is on that table.
5. But nobody has ever set a place there for you. Not one you could point at.
6. That's why you're not asking to move up a list, dear. You're asking to appear on one.
7. Let me look closer at what stands between you and a place of your own…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of what is planned rather than what is left over.
2. You asked about the place you hold, and drew the card of intention, which is exactly where this needs looking.
3. Let me be straight with you, dear, because I think you have had enough gentle evasions. A man's children do have a first call on him. That is not an injustice done to you and I will not pretend otherwise to make this evening easier. So you will hear no such thing from this card as 'you ought to come first'. But look at what it IS the card of. Planning. Intention. Things put in a diary on purpose. Children coming first has never once meant that the woman who loves him gets whatever remains when everything else is finished. Those are two entirely different arrangements, dear. First call is not the same as only call. You are not asking to be moved up a list. You are asking to appear on one.
4. Let me look closer at what has actually been planned for you…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — his hands are behind his back, where you can't see them.
2. You asked why his children come before you. Your hand went to the man hung the other way up.
3. So the race was handed to you, dear. You never entered it yourself.
4. And his children do come first, dear. That part is true and it stays true.
5. But first was never the same as only, dear. Nobody drew you a place either way.
6. That's why you can't name where you sit, dear. No one has ever told you.
7. Let me look closer at what has been standing where your place should be…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card that turns a question over and shows you its underside.
2. Your hand found the card of the reversal, and there is one here that I would like you to see.
3. You have asked why they come before you — notice what that question has done, dear. It has placed you and those children on a single ladder with one rung worth having, and set you to competing for it. I do not think that ladder is yours. I think it was handed to you: by an arrangement in which you are always the one who adjusts, by every plan that moved, and possibly not by him at all. You are not in a contest with children, dear. You were never going to win one. The Hanged Man turns it into a quieter question, and a fairer one. Not whether you rank above them. Where you sit at all — named, reliable, somewhere a person could point to.
4. Let me look closer at where you actually sit in all this…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — his coat is covered in flowers, and the drop is one step away.
2. You asked why his children come before you. Your hand went to the one who walked in late.
3. So you came into a life that was already full, dear. You came with open hands.
4. And a full life leaves very little room showing, dear. That's what you walked into.
5. But wanting a place of your own takes nothing from theirs, dear.
6. That's why you've asked for so little, dear. You called that being fair.
7. Let me look closer at what has been in the way of your own place…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of the one who walked into it open-handed.
2. You came into a life that was already full, and drew the card of the woman who went in without conditions.
3. You arrived somewhere already occupied and you did it generously, dear. But the Fool has never pretended a thing costs nothing, so I will not either. Two things I decline outright. Grading that man as a father is the first — neither this card nor I have ever seen him with them. Pronouncing on what his children ought to mean to him is the second, since the answer there is everything. Wanting a place of your own is not the same as wanting theirs to be smaller. You may have told yourself they are, on the harder nights — but they are not, and believing they are is how a woman comes to ask for nothing whatever and call it being reasonable.
4. Let me look closer at what you have stopped letting yourself ask for…

</details>
