# `cards-honest` — "Is he being honest with you?"

⛔ The decode-him DECK was retired 2026-08-19 (operator call) — its strip art was the fb-palm thumb image and no ad ever pointed at it. Its block has been removed from this draft; the arcana and return-mhf decks are unaffected.

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why. 🔴 THIS FAMILY WAS DELIBERATELY NOT LOOSENED: cut 3 still answers her FEAR (am I short of sense, or short of facts) and never rules on him in either direction. The art line and the bridge are unchanged.

Lives on ALL FOUR decks — three card-sets: Magician/Hanged Man/Fool (return-mhf + arcana-mfh, one copy, verb swapped), Emperor/Empress/Fool (arcana-eef), Sun/Moon/Tower (decode-him). The arcana-eef Fool reuses the MHF Fool copy verb-swapped, which is what the live registry already does for this hook.

🔴 decode-him takes NO picture line. Its strip art is the fb-PALM thumb image and there is no revealStrip, so there is no card for her to look at — beat 1 uses the card-name form instead, exactly as the already-shipped cards-feels and cards-return do on that deck. Every beat 1 here is written FRESH: all four decode-him hooks currently open with the SAME line per card and no guard file catches it.

🔴 NEVER ACQUIT HIM AND NEVER CONVICT HIM. TAROT_HOOK_TENDENCY: 'never state he is lying or that he is honest as fact; affirm HER instrument'. The VOC makes that a safety rule rather than a style rule — 28 real concerns off this exact lander, and a large share of them have never met the man: 'I just want make sure he is not an impostor', 'he says he loves me... I haven't met him afraid he's a scammer', 'I met this guy online. He's from Nigeria... my gut feeling tells me he's not [coming]'. A line that vouches for him could cost one of these women money. So every beat 3 certifies HER NOTICING and names what she was never given — never a verdict in either direction.

The accusation the absolution refuses is hers from the VOC too: 'Left confused doubting our entire relationship with no answers', 'I don't know if I can trust him'. She has been told she is distrustful. She is short of facts.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — he looks straight out at you, and his mouth is closed.
2. You asked if he's being honest with you. Your hand went to the man who tells it well.
3. So what you're short of is a fact, dear. Not sense, and not calm.
4. And look how he holds himself. He's very good at how a thing is put.
5. But a smooth account isn't proof either way, dear.
6. That's why his answers land fine, dear, and you still go back over them.
7. Let me look closer at what stands between you and the whole story…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of the crafted image, of what's presented with skill.
2. Your hand didn't reach for that by accident; some part of you senses a gap between what he shows and what's underneath.
3. The Magician doesn't mean he's lying — it means he's practiced at presenting, and that sense the picture is a little too polished is real information, not you being difficult.
4. Let me look closer at what's behind the performance…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — the whole card is upright but him.
2. You asked if he's being honest with you. Your hand went to the card that shows one side.
3. So you've only ever had one side of this, dear. The card is blunt about that.
4. And the other side of it sits with him, dear. It always has.
5. But you can't weigh a thing you were never shown, dear.
6. That's why he explains it, dear, and an hour later you're still checking.
7. Let me look closer at what's keeping the other side out of view…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of what's suspended, held just out of view.
2. You reached for the card that matches the feeling you can't quite settle.
3. The Hanged Man doesn't mean he's lying — it means something between you is on pause, unresolved, and the sense that the full truth hasn't turned to face you yet is accurate.
4. Let me look closer at what's waiting to come into view…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — a small white dog up on its back legs beside him.
2. You asked if he's being honest with you. Your hand went to the one still on the road.
3. So this story isn't finished, dear. Nothing about it has been closed.
4. And look at the little dog, dear. Up on its back legs, trying to tell him.
5. But a warning with no words in it is all you've had, dear.
6. That's why you ask again in a good week, dear, when nothing has gone wrong.
7. Let me look closer at what has been standing in the way of the truth…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of the open, unguarded hand.
2. You reached for the card that matches something you feel in how he moves through this.
3. The Fool doesn't point to deception — it points to a man who hasn't sat down and thought it through; what's unsaid here may be unexamined rather than hidden, and your sense that you're not getting the whole picture is still accurate.
4. Let me look closer at what he's leaving unsaid…

</details>

# `cards-how-much-longer` — "How much longer will something keep blocking my money?"

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art. Written 2026-08-19 straight to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework, so bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain (so / and / but / that's why). Money translation: cut 3 answers her fear about HERSELF ("it was never you"), cut 4 says where the thing in the way actually sits, cut 5 is EARNING against KEEPING, cut 6 is the NEAR-MISS.

🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel, and NONE was loosened with the love families. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration, no deserving. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke.

📊 Built on the NEAR-MISS, not on poverty — docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md §3 says so outright ('The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty'): "I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish", "Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't". That clears ban 7 for free.

✅ GUARDED: tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts now exists and runs against these drafts directly (the drafts setup can only patch hooks that are already in the registry, so an unwired family would otherwise be checked by nothing at all).

Tendency: a block does not run down like a clock — it lasts as long as it is left alone, so the honest answer is about what gets moved, not a number. 🔴 NEVER give a date, a timeframe or a season, even a soft one.

# `cards-imagining-it` — "Does he love me, or am I imagining it?"

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework, after the interior ban was loosened. The read may now ANSWER: the card carries the claim about him. 🔴 THE DENIAL IS STILL BANNED — 'he does not love you' is the one direction she has no use for. 🔴 NO DIAGNOSIS: avoidant, emotionally unavailable, commitment-phobic, narcissist. 🔴 Never grade her (naive, foolish, gave too much). 🔴 No dates. The seven cuts run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why / let me look closer — and every cut must add information the last one did not carry. 🔴🔴 The crueller door stays shut: never that she imagined it, invented it or is seeing what she wants. 🔴 The warrant is the CARD, never the strength of what she felt.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the table is between you and the rest of him.
2. You asked about his love, and about your own eyes. Your hand went to the card of things truly made.
3. So you did not imagine it, dear. He loves you.
4. And this card builds nothing by chance. What stands between you was made on purpose.
5. But he has put it into the doing, dear, and not into words you can hold.
6. That's why you keep checking it against your own memory.
7. Let me look closer at what keeps him from saying it plainly…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of the thing that genuinely got made, whatever either of you calls it.
2. You asked whether you invented all this, and drew the card of what was actually built.
3. Two answers are available to me here, dear, and I am handing you neither one. What sits inside him is his to speak, and I intend to stay well out of it. Nor am I going to suggest that you conjured the thing yourself, because that is the crueller of the two and it is almost never true. Something happened between the pair of you. It had a shape, it took up real time, and you were present for every hour of it. The Magician deals in what was made rather than in who meant what by it, and whatever it turns out to have meant to him, it was not assembled out of thin air by a woman sitting on her own.
4. Let me look closer at what was actually built between you…

</details>

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

2. You asked about his love, and about your own eyes. Your hand went to the card of a crooked view.
3. So the love is there, dear. You have been looking at it sideways.
4. And from down there, every plain thing can look like something else.
5. But the angle is the trouble, dear. Your eyes are working fine.
6. That's why one week you are certain, and the next week you are not.
7. Let me look closer at what has been holding you at that angle…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

2. It was the card of the reversal your hand found, and there is one sitting inside what you asked me.
3. You have offered me two doors, dear, and I am walking through neither of them: not the one where he adores you, and not the one where you assembled all of this by yourself. The choice itself is doing you harm. Those are not the only two things that can be true, dear, and being handed only two is how a woman ends up doubting her own eyes. A thing can be real and unspoken. It can be real and not enough. It can be real to one person at one size and to the other at quite another. The Hanged Man's entire business is turning a question over, and turned over, yours stops being about his heart and becomes about why you were left with only two doors.
4. Let me look closer at why it came down to two doors…

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

2. You asked about his love, and about your own eyes. Your hand went to the card of a man who steps out anyway.
3. So it's real, dear. You did not build this on your own.
4. And he stepped out too. That is not a thing a man does over nothing.
5. But he stepped without once looking at where it lands.
6. That's why it feels real to you, dear, and unsteady at the same time.
7. Let me look closer at what makes the ground feel so unsteady…

2. You came asking me to rule on your own eyes, and drew the card of the woman who went ahead and believed.
3. I am not going to hand you the sentence you have braced for, dear — that none of it was there and you constructed the whole of it yourself. Women are told that far too easily and it does damage that outlasts the man. Nor will I swing the other way and promise you that anything felt strongly enough must therefore be returned in full; a feeling is not evidence, however large it is, and telling you otherwise would be flattery rather than help. The Fool sits with the harder and truer thing. You saw something, and you acted on it, and that was not foolish of you. What it came to on his side is still open — and you are allowed to want it said aloud rather than deduced.
4. Let me look closer at what you saw before the doubt arrived…

</details>

# `cards-left-without-word` — "Why did he leave without a word?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NEVER SUPPLY HIS REASON — not scared, not overwhelmed, not 'he couldn't cope', not 'he left because', not 'the reason he'. The reason lives in a man who did not give it, and inventing one hands her something false to act on. 🔴 Never a verdict on his person, 🔴 never invent a third person, and 🔴 note that 'it was never about you' and 'he was protecting you' are banned too — they are motive claims wearing reassurance. 🔴 No tactic in either direction: no reaching out, no one last message, no blocking, no no-contact. 🔴 No promise of closure.

Finding, kept distinct from cards-ghosted: the missing thing is not the reason, it is the SENTENCE. Somebody could have said one at any point, and the not-saying is a separate injury from the leaving.

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO VERDICT IN EITHER DIRECTION. These three name an untruth outright, so both answers are live harms: 'he lied to you' is an accusation against a real man on no evidence, and 'he is telling you the truth' is a reassurance the funnel has no standing to give — and on this audience the second is the more dangerous of the two. 🔴 Never blame her judgment: naive, gullible, too trusting, 'should have known' are all out. 🔴 No timeframe.

Finding, kept distinct from cards-truth: she has already checked her own read three times, and the checking is the tell. What can be affirmed is the instrument — not the finding it reached.

# `cards-live-apart` — "Why do we still live apart?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 now run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS instead of certifying her. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every family ban below still stands; this family was NOT loosened.

🔴 NEVER SUPPLY A CAUSE for the separate homes — not that he is not serious, not that he is protecting himself, not money, not children, not an ex. The reason lives in him, and inventing one hands her something to act on. Its own test guard sweeps for exactly that. Never read the arrangement as a verdict on his feelings in either direction, never a timeframe, never an ultimatum, never tell her to move in or move out.

VOC (678 V1 concerns on the distance/living-apart theme) shows she has already HEARD the reasons — that is the point. "I've asked him to move in, but 'he can't right now financially.'" "He wants us to continue to grow together... how long should I wait?" "There is something not quite right with his home life but he doesn't discuss it with me." Every reason she has been given arrived without a date. So the read refuses the 'why' (his to give) and answers the shape instead: a plan and a habit look identical from outside, and nobody has told her which one she lives in. The absolution is the one she has never been offered — she has been doing the work of two people, living the arrangement AND translating it afterwards.

# `cards-losing-interest` — "Is he losing interest, or just going through something?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO VERDICT EITHER WAY — 'he is losing interest' convicts him, 'he still loves you' reassures on his behalf, and 'nothing has gone wrong' is the same reassurance in a smaller coat. 🔴 NO CHASE STRATEGY, which is what the whole internet sells against this headline: no giving him space, no pulling back, no matching his energy, no going quiet so he chases. 🔴 Never blame her — nothing about being too much, too eager, moving too fast, or what she should have held back.

🔴 ITS HEADLINE IS AN EITHER-OR AND BOTH HALVES ARE FORBIDDEN ANSWERS. The test requires every card to VISIBLY refuse the binary — and to never resolve it in passing either. The finding: the choice is not hers to make and was never hers to carry, and she has been running both branches at once for weeks.

# `cards-love-again` — "Will I love again?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why. This is a SELF-FRAME hook, so the hopeful yes is affirmed with certainty; the DATE stays banned. The art line and the bridge are unchanged.

Face-UP decks only — arcana-mfh (Magician/Hanged Man/Fool) and arcana-eef (Emperor/Empress/Fool), so she CHOSE the card rather than turned it, and two card-sets means two sets of copy. The Fool copy is shared between them, verb already correct.

⭐ THESE ARE THE ONLY TWO HOOKS ON THE FUNNEL THAT READ HER RATHER THAN HIM. SELF_FRAME_HOOKS swaps 'tendency, never a verdict' for 'affirm the hopeful yes with certainty' — which is safe here precisely because no specific man exists to be convicted or acquitted. So these are warmer than everything else in this batch, on purpose.

⚠️ SEED FAMILY WITH NO GUARD FILE (`tests/tarot-self-frame-copy.test.ts` does not exist) — only the generic guards apply. 🔴 Still never tie it to one named person and 🔴 never give a date. cards-soulmate's tendency permits the 'when' to be answered only as a LEANING, never as a timeframe.

Finding: she is not really asking about a future person — she is asking whether the part of her that loves has been used up. It has not, and this is one of the few landers on the funnel allowed to say so plainly.

**NEW — what she would read**

2. You asked if you'll love again. Your hand went to the man who can still make things.
3. So yes, dear. The part of you that loves isn't used up.
4. And the roses are out again, dear. They come back every winter.
5. But you've been told otherwise, dear. I don't think that was true.
6. That's why you look yourself over for damage, dear. There's none to find.

# `cards-love-or-moved-on` — "Does he still love me, or has he moved on?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NEVER NARRATE HIS INTERIOR — the ban list here is the longest on the funnel and it covers both directions: 'he still loves you' promises the love survived, 'he has moved on' issues the burial, and 'he still thinks about you', 'you cross his mind', 'a part of him always will', 'deep down he' are all the softened confession. 🔴 No directive ('time to let go'), 🔴 no pathologising her ('obsessed', 'stuck', 'clinging', 'can't let go'), and 🔴 nothing presuming HOW he went — not that he chose to walk out, not that he died.

🔴 THE FAMILY'S OWN FINDING, pinned, and it belongs to no other hook: the two branches are NOT OPPOSITES. A person can carry something and still walk on. If this ever drifts to 'neither is knowable' it has become cards-moved-on and the comparison is dead — so it may not restate that incumbent's grounds either.

⚠️ Beat 2 could not restate her headline: 'he still loves you' is itself a banned phrase, so the echo had to be rebuilt as 'if the love is there, or if he has moved past it'.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — a red rose and a white lily grow in the same bed.
2. You asked if the love is there, or if he has moved past it. Your hand went to the man who sorts things.
3. So what you had was real, dear. That much isn't in question.
4. And look at the bed, dear. Rose and lily, side by side, both real.
5. But love and moving on were never a pair, dear.
6. That's why no answer has fitted, dear. The question was built wrong.
7. Let me look closer at what put that false choice in front of you…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of what actually gets done, as against what is merely felt.
2. You handed me two options and drew the card that separates them, which is a better piece of luck than you know.
3. Look at the two things you have set either side of that little word 'or', dear, because they are not opposites. Moving on is something a person DOES — mornings, work, other faces at the table. Loving is not done at all; it happens inside somebody, with nothing to show for it. A man can have built an entire new life and still carry you in him, or be sitting perfectly still and feel nothing whatever. The Magician cannot pick between them because they were never a pair to pick from. You have been reading every scrap of him as evidence for one of two verdicts, and it has been impossible — you thought that was your failing, and it is not. Notice what neither door touches: the thing you had was real when you had it.
4. Let me look closer at the question you were handed…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — one leg goes up and one goes across, at the same time.
2. You asked if the love is there, or if he has moved past it. Your hand went to the card that holds two things.
3. So he can hold both, dear. People do it all the time.
4. And look at him. Up and down at once, and both are true.
5. But you were handed a choice that doesn't work, dear. Not your doing.
6. That's why picking one has felt wrong, dear. Neither is the true answer.
7. Let me look closer at what has been forcing this into two…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of the question asked from the wrong end.
2. Your hand went to the card of the reversal, and yours needs turning about as much as any I have seen.
3. Your question has room in it for exactly one person, dear, and it is not you. Both doors lead to him: what he feels, what he has done — and everything that happens next in your life apparently waits behind that answer. I am not going to walk through either door. The inside of a man is not visible to me, and anyone who tells you otherwise is selling you something. What the Hanged Man asks instead is the question nobody has put to you in all this time. Not what he feels. What YOU want — and whether you would even recognise the answer now. I am not telling you to stop asking, dear. I am saying your own answer has been at the back of the queue behind his for a long while.
4. Let me look closer at the question nobody has asked you…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — his stick is behind him and the flower is in front.
2. You asked if the love is there, or if he has moved past it. Your hand went to the one going forward.
3. So he holds the old and the new at once, dear. People are like that.
4. And look at his hands. Stick behind him, flower out in front.
5. But nobody made you pick one, dear. The question did that.
6. That's why the two answers keep swapping places, dear, night after night.
7. Let me look closer at what has been making you pick…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of what has not been decided.
2. You came for one of two verdicts and drew the card that refuses to issue either.
3. You have braced for one sentence and hoped for the other, dear, and I am giving you neither. Not 'he still loves you' — I would be inventing that, and you would live off it until it ran out and left you exactly here again, only tireder. Not 'he has moved on' — that is a burial for a living man, performed by a woman who has never met him. The Fool's whole business is the road that is still open, and this one is still open. But open does not mean promised, it does not mean hopeless, and it does not mean you must stand at the roadside until somebody comes to tell you which.
4. Let me look closer at what is still open, and what never was…

</details>

# `cards-loyal` — "Is he loyal to only me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why. 🔴 NOT LOOSENED: no verdict on a third person in EITHER direction, so cut 3 answers her FEAR (was something real taken from me, or am I making it up) and never rules on him. Each hook keeps its own finding — GAP / PORTION / ATTENTION / REST — which its guard file pins in beat 3.

Face-down only (return-mhf) — the family is pinned off the face-up decks.

🔴 THE FLAGGED WORD (cheat / cheating / affair / infidelity) MAY NOT APPEAR IN ANY BEAT. 🔴 And the paranoia frame is banned in BOTH directions — no 'paranoid', 'imagining it', 'overthinking', 'insecure', 'crazy'. Calling her it is the injury; using it to reassure her plants it. That removes the absolution form used everywhere else on the funnel, so each of these names the accusation in other words.

🔴 Never assert a third person and never deny one. Never instruct her to check a phone, gather proof, or confront him — the most available wrong answer to these questions anywhere on the internet. Never excuse him, never land it as her fault. Four separate findings, or the family is one lander wearing four headlines.

Finding: she has taken a PORTION for the whole, and has been quietly adjusting herself around its size.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — he's the only figure on his card.
2. You asked if he's loyal to only you. Your hand went to the man with it all laid out.
3. So you've been given a share, dear, and you know its size.
4. And look at his table. Each thing on it has been counted out.
5. But a share isn't the whole, dear. You've felt where it stops.
6. That's why you can be right beside him, dear, and still be doing sums.
7. Let me look closer at what keeps the rest of it out of reach…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of what a person actually spends, as against what they say they have.
2. Your hand found the card of the ledger, and there is a reckoning in your question that you have not let yourself do.
3. I will not tell you where anything of his goes, and no card can produce that accounting — what the Magician turns over is your own word, the small one: 'only'. That word is not about a rival. It is about whether what reaches you is the whole of what he has, and you have been quietly answering that for yourself for some time by accepting a portion and calling it the amount. Nothing about noticing the difference makes you demanding.
4. Let me look closer at the portion you have been calling the whole…

</details>

2. You asked if he's loyal to only you. Your hand went to the card that holds part of it back.
3. So part of this is held back, dear. You've felt that for a while.
4. And look at him. Fixed there, giving out no more than he has to.
5. But you've been taking the part for the whole, dear. Anyone would.
6. That's why a good day never quite covers it. The rest is still missing.
7. Let me look closer at what's holding the rest of it back…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

2. You reached for the card of the withheld share, and a share is precisely what your question is about.
3. The Hanged Man keeps no roster of anybody, and naming a name would simply be me making one up — what it suspends is the assumption that this is a question about somebody else at all. It may not be. A person can be entirely where they are supposed to be and still keep a portion of themselves out of reach, and being given most of someone is a particular kind of lonely, because there is nothing in it you are allowed to complain about. You have not been imagining the missing part.
4. Let me look closer at the part that has been kept back…

</details>

4. And look at him. Nothing he carries is tied down.

6. That's why you call it keeping the peace, dear, and it still costs you.

What you asked for in that question is not extravagant: to be the whole of somebody's answer rather than most of it. You have been treating that as a large thing to want, and adjusting yourself downward to fit what you were actually receiving. The adjusting is the part I want to look at, because you have stopped noticing you are doing it.
4. Let me look closer at the adjusting you have stopped noticing…

</details>

# `cards-meant-alone` — "Am I meant to be alone?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO FATE, IN ANY FORM — 'you are meant to be alone' is the most harmful sentence available on this funnel, and 'it is meant to be', 'the universe has a plan', 'this is preparing you', 'there is a reason for this' are the same sentence in kinder clothes: they make her suffering purposeful and assign it to her. 🔴 NO LIFE SENTENCE and 🔴 NO PROMISE — 'you will always be alone' and 'you will meet someone' are both strangers ruling on the rest of her life. 🔴 No pathologising: nothing about her being negative, bitter, closed off, self-sabotaging, or 'attracting what she puts out'.

Finding: it refuses the PREMISE that anything is being assigned at all — not just the answer. Being alone is a circumstance, not a designation, and nobody wrote it down against her name.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — one hand points up and the other points at the flowers.
2. You came asking about being meant to be alone. Your hand went to the man who decides for himself.
3. So nothing was assigned to you, dear. There was no one to assign it.
4. And he makes the thing himself, dear. It wasn't handed to him.
5. But being alone now is a fact about now, dear. It isn't a label.
6. That's why meant has stuck to you, dear. Someone else said it first.
7. Let me look closer at what has been standing in for a reason…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of the maker, which is a different thing entirely from the made.
2. The card that came up under your question is the one about authorship, and authorship is precisely what your question assumes.
3. There is no one assigning you a part and the Magician does not hand out roles — I will not tell you that you are meant for solitude and I will not tell you that you are meant for company, because 'meant' would require somebody to have decided, and nothing has decided anything about you. Your question quietly supposes that a circumstance is a designation. It is not one. What has happened to you so far is not an instruction about what you are for.
4. Let me look closer at the instruction you believe you were given…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — the tree he hangs from has bark still on it.
2. You came asking about being meant to be alone. Your hand went to the card hung the other way up.
3. So nobody decided this for you, dear. Not one person.
4. And he hangs there, dear, and no one chose that for him either.
5. But you've been living as though a choice was made, dear.
6. That's why there is nothing here to accept, dear, and nothing to fight.
7. Let me look closer at what has been sitting where that author was…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of the thing seen from below and mistaken for the whole.
2. Yours was the card of the reversed picture, and this question has sat reversed in you a long time.
3. No fate is written in the Hanged Man, and I would not read one out even if it were — what it turns over is the direction of your question. You have been asking whether you were singled out. Nobody is singled out, because nobody is being assigned, and that is a colder comfort than being told you are destined for something lovely. It is also the true one. There is no ruling on you here to be appealed against, because no ruling was ever entered.
4. Let me look closer at the ruling you believe was entered…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

3. So no fate is written on the Fool, dear. None is written on you.
4. And he's out on open ground, dear, with no path marked at all.
5. But a sentence has been sitting on you, dear. Nobody passed one.
6. That's why it feels settled, dear, when nothing has been settled at all.

Whatever you have been reading as a sentence passed upon you is a stretch of circumstance, and circumstance says nothing whatever about a person. None of your being on your own has been a judgement. You have been carrying it as though it were one, which is a far heavier thing than the circumstance itself.
4. Let me look closer at the sentence you have been reading over yourself…

# `cards-met-already` — "Have I already met my soulmate without realizing it?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 THE LABEL BAN RUNS BOTH WAYS — never certify that he is her soulmate or twin flame, and never deny it. It is unfalsifiable, so she could never check it against anything he actually does, and it is a verdict on a real man. 🔴🔴 NEVER RANK THE LABELS: no word here is higher, rarer or deeper than another, and 'just a strong connection' may never be the losing branch. 🔴 No runner script, no separation phase, no ascension homework, no tactic, no timeframe. ⭐ The shared move: affirm the PULL as real information about HER, and refuse the WORD.

🔴 WIRING NOTE — READ BEFORE SHIPPING THIS FAMILY. tests/tarot-soulmate-label-copy.test.ts:439/441/444 assume ONE UNSPLIT beat 3 (openerB length 5, msgs[3] is the loop line, msgs[2].length > 400). The 7-bubble shape splits beat 3, so those three assertions must be DELETED with a note when this family is wired — not worked around. Nothing else in that file needs touching.

🔴 NEVER POINT AT ANYBODY. This headline invites the reading to send her back through her own past hunting for a face — 'you already know who', 'think back to', 'the one who came before' are all banned, and so is grading her past self for missing him. Finding: the fear underneath is that she has already spent her one chance, and the read answers that premise rather than the search.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — nothing on his table has been used up.
2. You asked if you've already met your soulmate. Your hand went to the man with full hands.
3. So there was never one go, dear. That isn't how any of this works.
4. And nothing on his table has been used up, dear. Nothing crossed off.
5. But you've been afraid you spent your one chance, dear.
6. That's why you keep sorting old faces, dear, hunting the one you missed.
7. Let me look closer at what put that one-chance idea in you…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of recognition, of knowing the thing when it is standing in front of you.
2. You asked whether you have already walked past something, and drew the card of noticing.
3. Two things will not be happening this evening, dear. Nobody is going to be pointed at — no face from your past, no name, nothing you could go and look up tonight. A name conjured at this table would send you back through years of your own messages hunting for a sign that was never in them. And you are not going to be told that you missed him. That is the sentence you half came here expecting, and this card has no intention of supplying it. 'Without realizing it' assumes a moment when the information sat there and you failed to read it. But a meeting is not an examination, dear, and noticing is a real faculty — yours is plainly in working order.
4. Let me look closer at what you have been afraid you walked past…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — he's looking at the world from the other end.
2. You asked if you've already met your soulmate. Your hand went to the card that looks again.
3. So a review isn't the same as a mistake found, dear.
4. And he sees the same world, dear, just from the other end of it.
5. But you've been searching your own past like a case file, dear.
6. That's why nothing you find settles it. You were doing your best, dear.
7. Let me look closer at what has you searching your own past…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of the thing that can only be seen properly from the other side.
2. You asked about hindsight, and drew the card that deals in nothing else at all.
3. Here is what hindsight does, dear, and it does it to all of us. It goes back through the past, quietly folds in everything you have learned since, then presents the result as though it had been lying in plain view at the time. It never was. You could only ever have known what you knew then, and a woman cannot be held to account for failing to read a page that had not yet been written. So no charges will be filed against your younger self here. A question like yours comes when the road ahead has gone quiet, and the mind goes back through the archive looking for where the mistake must have been.
4. Let me look closer at why this question has come now…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the road he's on hasn't been walked before.
2. You asked if you've already met your soulmate. Your hand went to the one with road ahead.
3. So there's road ahead of you, dear. Plainly, and plenty of it.
4. And all of it is unwalked, dear. He faces forward the whole time.
5. But a meeting behind you wouldn't close a road like that, dear.
6. That's why the fear doesn't hold, dear, whichever way the answer goes.
7. Let me look closer at what has been narrowing the road for you…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of the road that carries on well past the part you can see.
2. You asked whether the road is behind you, and drew the card whose whole nature is what has not happened yet.
3. Your door stays open, dear, and I want it clear that this is a refusal running in both directions rather than a kindness. You will not be told it has already happened and slipped by — that would be inventing a loss for you to grieve, and you would grieve it, on far less than a card. Nor will you be promised an arrival, because a promise of that kind is a date wearing a disguise. What the Fool takes issue with is buried underneath the question: you have asked it as though your life were a single-question examination you may already have failed. A life is not an examination, dear.
4. Let me look closer at what you have been treating as a test you already failed…

</details>

# `cards-misled` — "Am I being misled?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

On the two Magician/Hanged Man/Fool decks — one set of copy, verb swapped.

⚠️ TRUST IS A SEED FAMILY WITH NO GUARD FILE (`tests/tarot-trust-copy.test.ts` does not exist). Only the generic guards apply, so this is among the least protected copy on the funnel and the bans below come from TAROT_HOOK_TENDENCY rather than from a test.

Finding: the MISMATCH between what she has been told and what she has observed is real information, and the confusion is not a flaw in her judgment. 🔴 Never state he is deceiving her, and 🔴 never tell her she is imagining it — both are available and both are wrong. What gets affirmed is her perception and her right to trust her own eyes.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the four things on his table don't match each other.
2. You asked if you're being misled. Your hand went to the man who arranges things.
3. So two things you were given don't fit, dear. You saw that yourself.
4. And there on his table, four things, and no two of them alike.
5. But a mismatch tells you nothing about why, dear. Only that it's there.
6. That's why you keep going back over what he said, lining it up.
7. Let me look closer at what stands between you and a story that fits…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of the hand that shapes the story.
2. You reached for the card of direction, and women do not reach for that card when the account they have been given adds up.
3. The Magician does not hand down a verdict that you are being deceived — it says the version of events you keep being offered has been shaped for you, and the small places where it does not match what you saw with your own eyes are exactly where your attention belongs.
4. Let me look closer at the detail that never quite fits…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — from where you stand, his face is where his feet should be.
2. You asked if you're being misled. Your hand went to the card that flips a view.
3. So the muddle is in the picture, dear. It isn't in you.
4. And you've been handed this the wrong way up, dear.
5. But nobody has turned it round for you, dear. You've had to do it.
6. That's why working it out takes you hours. It arrives upside down, dear.
7. Let me look closer at what has been turning this around on you…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of the view that is deliberately kept unclear.
2. You reached for the card that matches the dizziness of never being able to settle what is true.
3. The Hanged Man is not proof that you are being played — it says you have been kept at a distance from the whole of it, and the confusion you have been blaming on yourself is a symptom of that distance, not a flaw in your judgment.
4. Let me look closer at what the confusion has been protecting…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the rocks he stands on are cut in flat layers.
2. You asked if you're being misled. Your hand went to the one who doesn't check his footing.
3. So you've been asked to walk without checking, dear. That's what feels wrong.
4. And he's one step from a drop, dear, and looking the other way.
5. But this card names nothing about what sits under it, dear.
6. That's why checking felt like doubt to you. Checking is just looking, dear.
7. Let me look closer at what has been hidden under this…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of what gets left uncorrected.
2. That is not random; you reached for the card of the easy answer, the one that was simpler to leave standing than to fix.
3. The Fool does not mean he set out to mislead you — it points to someone who let a convenient impression stand rather than correct it, which lands on you the same way in the end; what you are sensing is a real absence of straightening-out, not you being suspicious for no reason.
4. Let me look closer at what he has never bothered to correct…

</details>

# `cards-money-wont-stay` — "What does my energy say about why money won't stay?"

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art. Written 2026-08-19 straight to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework, so bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain (so / and / but / that's why). Money translation: cut 3 answers her fear about HERSELF ("it was never you"), cut 4 says where the thing in the way actually sits, cut 5 is EARNING against KEEPING, cut 6 is the NEAR-MISS.

🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel, and NONE was loosened with the love families. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration, no deserving. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke.

📊 Built on the NEAR-MISS, not on poverty — docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md §3 says so outright ('The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty'): "I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish", "Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't". That clears ban 7 for free.

✅ GUARDED: tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts now exists and runs against these drafts directly (the drafts setup can only patch hooks that are already in the registry, so an unwired family would otherwise be checked by nothing at all).

Tendency: the drawing-in half has always worked and what is at issue is everything after it arrives. 🔴 NEVER name who or what takes it, never call her careless with money, and never advise her on keeping it.

3. So the getting has always worked, dear. That half of it is sound.
4. And this card is all about bringing it in, dear. Look at the table.
5. But drawing it in is one job, dear. Keeping it is a second one.
6. That's why it arrives and goes, dear, and you never see it settle.
7. Let me look closer at the door it goes back out of…

# `cards-moved-on` — "Is he coming back, or has he moved on?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO FORECAST IN EITHER DIRECTION — 'he will come back' and 'he has moved on' are both things no card knows, and the second is the one that sounds like honesty. 🔴 Never blame her for waiting: naive, too trusting, foolish, clinging, desperate and 'wasted your time' are all banned outright. 🔴 No timeframe, no guarantee.

🔴 THE HEADLINE HANDS OVER AN EITHER-OR AND ANSWERING EITHER HALF IS THE FAILURE, so every card must visibly decline it. Its own grounds, kept distinct from cards-love-or-moved-on: the pair was manufactured by the WAITING, not by anything he did — she built two boxes because two boxes are easier to live in than an open question.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — a gold cup, a coin, and two long things beside them.
2. You asked about coming back, or moving on. Your hand went to the man who lays things out.
3. So I will not pick either one, dear. Neither box is filled yet.
4. And four things lie on that wood, dear, and none above another.
5. But those two boxes came out of long waiting, dear. Not out of him.
6. That's why sorting him has never worked, dear. He hasn't been sorted.
7. Let me look closer at what has kept this question open…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of the move that is made on purpose, never by drift.
2. Your hand reached for the card of agency, and for a question with two answers in it that matters more than which answer.
3. The Magician refuses the either-or you brought me — it says that whichever of the two turns out to be true, it will be something he does rather than something that merely happens to him, and you are owed the telling of it instead of being left to work it out from silence.
4. Let me look closer at what his silence has been doing in place of an answer…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — the light behind his head doesn't touch the rest of him.
2. You asked about coming back, or moving on. Your hand went to the card that refuses to land.
3. So this card declines to land, dear. So will I.
4. And he's between, dear, and content to stay between.
5. But neither of your boxes has anything in it yet, dear.
6. That's why the two swap places all day, dear. Hour by hour.
7. Let me look closer at what has been holding this between…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of two answers that have not yet come apart.
2. You reached for the card of the unseparated, which is exactly the position he has left you standing in.
3. The Hanged Man will not divide your question for me — it says both halves of it are still live, and being made to hold two opposite futures open at the same time is genuinely exhausting; that exhaustion is the price of not being told, not proof that you are unable to let go.
4. Let me look closer at what it is costing you to hold both…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the sky is gold and there's not one cloud in it.
2. You asked about coming back, or moving on. Your hand went to the one who files nobody.
3. So the Fool will not sort him for you, dear. Nor will I.
4. And he's still walking, dear, with nothing filed behind him.
5. But that answer sits with one person, dear, and he hasn't given it.
6. That's why you built two boxes, dear. Standing in nothing is hard.
7. Let me look closer at what has kept you standing between them…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card that refuses to be pinned to one answer.
2. That is not random; you reached for the card of what has not yet hardened into anything.
3. The Fool declines to take a side of your question — it points to a thing still unfixed rather than one already settled behind your back, and the not-knowing you have been carrying as your own indecision was never yours; it is his, and it has been sitting in your lap.
4. Let me look closer at whose uncertainty you have been carrying…

</details>

# `cards-nest-egg` — "How long has something been blocking me from a nest egg?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Money translation: cut 3 answers her fear about HERSELF ("it was never you"), cut 4 says where the thing in the way actually sits, cut 5 is EARNING against KEEPING, cut 6 is the NEAR-MISS. 🔴 NONE of the seven bans below was loosened with the love families — this is the one place where a wrong sentence costs her actual money.

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art.

🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke.

✅ GUARDED as of 2026-08-19: `tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts` now exists and runs against these drafts DIRECTLY. It had to: the drafts setup can only patch a hook the registry already has, so an unwired family is checked by nothing at all and still prints a green tick. All seven bans were fed a deliberate violation and all seven fired.

📊 WHAT THE VOC CHANGED. These hooks have never run, so there is no per-hook data. The nearest real corpus is docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md — 10,514 V1 money concerns. Two themes matter here and they pull in different directions.
**Retirement (554 concerns, the highest earner in the bucket at $15,146/1k):** the emotion is not panic, it is quiet arithmetic dread. 'I'm worried that I'll outlive my few savings.' 'Prices are going up but my pension is not.' 'I thought I would be set by now.'
**The invisible block (1,208 concerns) — and its instruction is explicit: 'The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty.'** 'I keep trying to get ahead and no matter what I do nothing seems to work out, it's like something is holding me back.' 'I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish.' 'Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't.'
So these reads are built on the NEAR-MISS, not on being short of money. That also satisfies ban 7 for free — nothing here presumes she is broke, only that she has got close and watched it go.

Tendency: long-standing rather than recent, and steady effort that never accumulates points at something in the way rather than at a failing in her. 🔴 NEVER give a length of time — the headline asks for one and it may not be supplied. Never tell her she should have saved differently.

# `cards-new-soulmate` — "Will I find a new soulmate after loss?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 THE ONLY FAMILY ON THE FUNNEL WHERE THE MAN MAY BE DEAD.

🔴🔴 MEDIUMSHIP is the failure mode that exists nowhere else here: 'he is at peace', 'he is watching over you', 'he would want you to be happy', 'he sent you here'. Every one is the most natural sentence in the world to say to a grieving woman, and every one is contact with the dead — a different product with a different compliance surface. server/lib/universalSafety.ts does NOT catch it. None of these reads speaks for him, reports his state, or grants his blessing.

🔴 NO ARRIVAL PROMISE and no timeframe — this family runs under AFTER_LOSS_TAROT_HOOKS rather than the self-frame 'affirm the hopeful yes with CERTAINTY' clause, because aimed at a bereaved partner that clause promises a replacement. 🔴 NEVER GRADE HER GRIEF: no 'you are ready', no 'you are not ready', no 'it is too soon', no 'you need more time'. 🔴 No grief directives and never rank the two loves.

⚠️ AND NOTHING MAY PRESUME A DEATH EITHER. The VOC shows 'loss' here is often something else entirely — 'My husband walked out after 21 yrs like I didn't matter at all', 'Husband in a nursing home', 'We broke up a month ago'. The reads stay agnostic about what the loss was, which also keeps them safe if it was a death.

Finding: the fear underneath is NOT whether such a person exists. It is whether loving again would mean replacing him. VOC, verbatim: 'I'm still going through the grief of losing my husband, so I'm not ready to find love again.' A generic 'yes there is hope' is the incumbent lander and answers nobody.

# `cards-not-enough` — "Was I not enough for him to stay?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NEVER SUPPLY HIS REASON — not scared, not overwhelmed, not 'he couldn't cope', not 'he left because', not 'the reason he'. The reason lives in a man who did not give it, and inventing one hands her something false to act on. 🔴 Never a verdict on his person, 🔴 never invent a third person, and 🔴 note that 'it was never about you' and 'he was protecting you' are banned too — they are motive claims wearing reassurance. 🔴 No tactic in either direction: no reaching out, no one last message, no blocking, no no-contact. 🔴 No promise of closure.

🔴 THE COMPARISON IS REFUSED, NEVER SCORED — and that includes the kind direction. 'You were more than enough' feels generous and is still a claim about why he went, which is a claim nobody here can make. So the read declines to weigh her at all, and names the scale itself as the thing that was handed to her.

# `cards-on-my-mind` — "Why is he always on my mind?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Finding: the word 'always'. She is reporting a FREQUENCY and asking to be told what it means about her. It means she has had nowhere to put it — an unfinished thing takes up room, and room is not the same as feeling.

4. Let me look closer at how much of you is still being held there…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

3. So some things follow you, dear. You didn't invite them.
4. And the dog came along, dear, and it stays along.
5. But you've been ordering yourself to stop, dear. That never works.
6. That's why it keeps pace with you, dear, however fast you go.

# `cards-pulling-away` — "Why is he pulling away from me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO VERDICT EITHER WAY — 'he is losing interest' convicts him, 'he still loves you' reassures on his behalf, and 'nothing has gone wrong' is the same reassurance in a smaller coat. 🔴 NO CHASE STRATEGY, which is what the whole internet sells against this headline: no giving him space, no pulling back, no matching his energy, no going quiet so he chases. 🔴 Never blame her — nothing about being too much, too eager, moving too fast, or what she should have held back.

Finding: she is being asked to interpret a change nobody explained, and the interpreting is the exhausting part — not the distance itself.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — his right hand is up and his left hangs open and low.
2. You asked why he's pulling away from you. Your hand went to the man who acts on purpose.
3. So something did change, dear. The card shows the shift plainly.
4. And look at his hands, dear. One reaching up, one letting go low.
5. But the reason sits with him, dear, and he hasn't said it out loud.
6. That's why guessing has taken up your weeks, dear. It's hard work.
7. Let me look closer at what has opened up between you…

2. Your hand went to the card of the choice made off-stage, and for a question like yours that is worth sitting with.
3. The Magician names nothing of what he has settled on, nor whether he has settled anything at all — it says the pulling back you have been measuring is real and not a thing you invented, and that whatever sits behind it is being worked out somewhere you were never given a way in. That is why turning it over on your own has produced no answer; the missing piece was never on your side of it.
4. Let me look closer at what is being decided out of your sight…

3. So you're halfway, dear. Not ended, and not held either.
4. And nobody has cut him down, dear. He just hangs there.
5. But you're being asked to read a gap with no words in it, dear.
6. That's why evenings leave you spent, dear. You've translated all day.
7. Let me look closer at what has been widening this…

3. The Hanged Man rules on neither of you — it marks a thing that stopped moving rather than a thing that stopped, and from where you are standing those two feel identical while meaning entirely different things. What you are living inside is the not-moving, and being kept there without a word of explanation is a weight of its own, quite apart from whatever the reason turns out to be.
4. Let me look closer at where the movement went out of this…

</details>

# `cards-ready-commit` — "Is he ever going to be ready for real commitment?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

On the two Magician/Hanged Man/Fool decks — one set of copy, verb swapped ('turned' face-down, 'chose' face-up).

🔴 THESE ASK FOR A PREDICTION OUTRIGHT, which is the exact thing the funnel forbids, so the no-verdict rule runs in BOTH directions plus a no-timeframe rule: 'he will, give it time' reads as a promise and 'he never will' is a pronouncement on a man she loves. 🔴 The bans in tests/tarot-commitment-copy.test.ts are WHOLE-BEAT rather than clause-level, so 'not enough' and 'too much' are unavailable even inside a refusal — the usual absolution form had to be rebuilt without them.

Finding: the GAP between what SHE is ready for and where HE is standing is real information — and her readiness is legitimate rather than impatience. Kept deliberately distinct from cards-twin-ready, which is its measured pair in tests/tarot-twin-flame-copy.test.ts and may share no 6-word run with it.

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You chose the Magician, dear. Look — the table hides him from the waist down.
2. You asked if he will be ready for real commitment. Your hand went to the man who builds.
3. So he will get there, dear. This card is a man who builds things.
4. And the making of it is already in his hands, dear.
5. But the table hides him from the waist down. You can't see his footing.
6. That's why he can talk about a future, dear. He never steps toward it.
7. Let me look closer at what sits in the gap between you…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You chose the Magician, dear — the card of a man's own power to build something.
2. You are asking about readiness, and your hand found the card of capability rather than circumstance.
3. The Magician makes no promise that the day arrives — it says the material is all there and unassembled, and your instinct that he is capable of more than he has been giving is not wishful thinking.
4. Let me look closer at what he would have to put down first…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You chose the Hanged Man, dear. Look — one shoe points down, and the other points out sideways.
2. You asked if he will be ready for real commitment. Your hand went to the card that stops the clock.
3. So he has stopped in one spot, dear. Stopped is not finished.
4. And look at his feet. One shoe points down, one points off sideways.
5. But I won't name a day for you, dear. There's no day on this card.
6. That's why it stalls at the very same place, dear, every single time.
7. Let me look closer at what keeps stopping this at the same spot…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You chose the Hanged Man, dear — the card of the long pause that comes before a turn.
2. You reached for the card of the in-between, and readiness is exactly the thing that lives there.
3. The Hanged Man offers no when and no whether — it says he is mid-change rather than finished forming, and the patience you have spent went to something genuinely unfinished rather than to nothing at all.
4. Let me look closer at the turn he has not made…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You chose the Fool, dear. Look — a small circle floats above him, next to a big bright sun.
2. You asked if he will be ready for real commitment. Your hand went to the one who hasn't chosen.
3. So nothing has been chosen yet, dear. Nothing has been ruled out either.
4. And he's stepping out with no one thing in mind, dear.
5. But you know exactly what you're ready for. That's the difference here.
6. That's why you wait for him to catch up, dear. You got there first.
7. Let me look closer at what stands between you and a real choice…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You chose the Fool, dear — the card of the beginner, of the one still learning the road.
2. Your hand went to the card of the untested, which is a fair description of where he is standing.
3. The Fool is no sign that growing into it is beyond him — it points to someone earlier in the journey than you are rather than someone who cannot make it, and the distance you have been feeling between you is real and worth naming out loud.
4. Let me look closer at the distance between where you each stand…

</details>

# `cards-ready-to-love` — "Am I ready to love again after losing him?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 THE ONLY FAMILY ON THE FUNNEL WHERE THE MAN MAY BE DEAD.

🔴🔴 MEDIUMSHIP is the failure mode that exists nowhere else here: 'he is at peace', 'he is watching over you', 'he would want you to be happy', 'he sent you here'. Every one is the most natural sentence in the world to say to a grieving woman, and every one is contact with the dead — a different product with a different compliance surface. server/lib/universalSafety.ts does NOT catch it. None of these reads speaks for him, reports his state, or grants his blessing.

🔴 NO ARRIVAL PROMISE and no timeframe — this family runs under AFTER_LOSS_TAROT_HOOKS rather than the self-frame 'affirm the hopeful yes with CERTAINTY' clause, because aimed at a bereaved partner that clause promises a replacement. 🔴 NEVER GRADE HER GRIEF: no 'you are ready', no 'you are not ready', no 'it is too soon', no 'you need more time'. 🔴 No grief directives and never rank the two loves.

⚠️ AND NOTHING MAY PRESUME A DEATH EITHER. The VOC shows 'loss' here is often something else entirely — 'My husband walked out after 21 yrs like I didn't matter at all', 'Husband in a nursing home', 'We broke up a month ago'. The reads stay agnostic about what the loss was, which also keeps them safe if it was a death.

🔴 BOTH ANSWERS ARE BANNED — 'you are ready' and 'you are not ready' are each a stranger grading a widow's grief — so every card must VISIBLY DECLINE the headline rather than sidestep it. The finding that replaces the verdict: PERMISSION here is hers, and nobody else gets a say.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the wand he holds is short, and he grips it near the middle.
2. You asked about loving again after losing him. Your hand went to the man who builds rather than rules.
3. So I will not grade you on this, dear. Nobody gets to.
4. And look at his hands. They're for making things, not for measuring them.
5. But there's no standard here that anyone could hold you to, dear.
6. That's why the question keeps coming back, dear. The permission was yours all along.
7. Let me look closer at what has been standing between you and your own say…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of the thing nobody else can do on your behalf.
2. Your hand went to the card of what only its owner may decide, and you came here asking to be told.
3. The Magician will not grade you ready and it will not grade you unready, and I am not going to do it in its place — what it shows me is that readiness is not a mark somebody awards you once you have grieved correctly. There is no standard here that you are passing or failing, and no one who has met you is qualified to set one. You have been asking whether you are allowed. The permission you have been waiting on was never in anyone else's keeping, which is precisely why waiting for it has not worked.
4. Let me look closer at the permission you have been waiting for…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — his tunic hangs down toward his chin.
2. You asked about loving again after losing him. Your hand went to the card that won't hurry.
3. So the Hanged Man declines to rule on you, dear.
4. And look at him. Nobody is timing him, and nobody is timing you.
5. But you've been keeping a clock on yourself, dear. Someone handed it to you.
6. That's why you weigh it up again each morning. It was always yours to say.
7. Let me look closer at what has been putting a clock on you…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of the thing held between two states and belonging fully to neither.
2. You reached for the card of the in-between, and that describes where you are more truly than either answer you came here for.
3. The Hanged Man will not choose between the two answers you came for, and I am not going to choose between them either — a person is not ready or unready the way a door is open or shut. You can want company and want him back in the very same hour, and neither one cancels the other out. Being in both at once is not confusion, and it is not evidence that you have failed to heal; it is what carrying this actually looks like from inside. Being made to pick one has cost you more than the not-knowing ever did.
4. Let me look closer at the two things you have been holding at once…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — his weight is on his back foot, and the front one is out.
2. You asked about loving again after losing him. Your hand went to the one who steps without knowing.
3. So the Fool will not grade you either, dear.
4. And look at him. He goes without being certain in advance.
5. But nobody is sure before they step, dear. Not one person.
6. That's why you've read your own doubt as a no, dear. Doubt says nothing.
7. Let me look closer at what has been standing in the way of your own step…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of the step taken before the ground is ever certain.
2. That is not random; your hand found the card of the beginning made without guarantees, and a guarantee is the thing you have been waiting to be given.
3. The Fool issues no verdict on your readiness and I will not stand one in for it — what it observes is that nobody has ever been certain in advance, and that the certainty you are waiting to feel does not arrive ahead of time to give permission. That is not a push, and there is no timetable here you are behind on. It only means the sign you have been watching for will not come as a feeling of being finished, because that feeling is not how any of this works, for you or for anyone.
4. Let me look closer at the sign you have been waiting to feel…

</details>
