# `cards-real-person` — "Is he the real person, or just a picture?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

On the two Magician/Hanged Man/Fool decks — one set of copy, verb swapped.

⚠️ TRUST IS A SEED FAMILY WITH NO GUARD FILE (`tests/tarot-trust-copy.test.ts` does not exist). Only the generic guards apply, so this is among the least protected copy on the funnel and the bans below come from TAROT_HOOK_TENDENCY rather than from a test.

🔴🔴 REASSURANCE IS THE FAILURE MODE HERE, NOT THE SAFE OPTION. The 2026-07-10 buyer audit caught Evelyn reframing textbook scam markers as a genuine bond. The standing rule: never reassure her the bond is genuine, never vouch for him — and still never state as fact that he is fake, which is its own verdict. 78 real concerns off this lander are overwhelmingly online and unmet: 'I need to know if it is really him….that I am talking to', 'I am speaking with two men on line', 'he always tells me he loves me and misses me so much' followed by 'sometimes he just disappears'. What gets affirmed is HER CAUTION as wisdom, and her right to ask for something real before more of her heart goes in.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — his front is all you get; his back is off the card.
2. You asked if he's the real person or just a picture. Your hand went to the man who makes images.
3. So something has kept you from reaching him, dear. That's real, not nerves.
4. And a made picture can be very good, dear. Good enough to hold up.
5. But I won't tell you he's real, dear, and I won't call him false.
6. That's why you go quiet when a friend asks, dear. You've no proof either way.
7. Let me look closer at what stands between you and meeting the man…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of the made thing, of the image that someone assembled.
2. Your hand went to the card of construction, and that tells me you have already sensed how much of him arrives pre-arranged.
3. The Magician does not tell me he is fictional — it tells me that what you have been given is an image, and an image can be built by anyone; the fact that you cannot get past it to a person is information, not impatience on your part.
4. Let me look closer at how much of him exists off the screen…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — the beam holds him up, and you can't see what holds the beam.
2. You asked if he's the real person or just a picture. Your hand went to the card held up by something unseen.
3. So you're being asked to take this on trust, dear. All of it.
4. And what holds him up is off the card. You can't see it from here.
5. But nobody can check a thing they have never been shown, dear.
6. That's why it never quite settles, dear. Nothing has answered it.
7. Let me look closer at what's keeping this out of your reach…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of the thing that hangs, that never quite lands.
2. You reached for the card that matches the waiting you have been doing for him to become real.
3. The Hanged Man does not declare him an invention — it marks someone who stays permanently almost-here, always one obstacle short of meeting you, and that pattern is the answer you have been asking me for; a man who wants to be known finds a way to be reached.
4. Let me look closer at what always seems to come up right before he arrives…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — his face is turned up, so you never quite meet his eye.
2. You asked if he's the real person or just a picture. Your hand went to the one whose face you can't catch.
3. So you've been reaching for something that keeps moving, dear.
4. And he's always mid-step, dear. Never still long enough to be seen.
5. But the card shows me no face to check him against, dear.
6. That's why every plan to meet slides a week, dear. Something always comes up.
7. Let me look closer at what keeps this from getting real…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of the beginning that has not yet touched the ground.
2. That is not random; you reached for the card of something still weightless, still untested by real life.
3. The Fool does not brand him a stranger — it says what you have so far is a beginning that has never had to survive an ordinary afternoon together, and until it does, what you are holding is a promise rather than a person; your caution here is wisdom, not cynicism.
4. Let me look closer at what it would take to bring this into daylight…

</details>

# `cards-really-love` — "Does he really love me?"

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework, after the interior ban was loosened. The read may now ANSWER: the card carries the claim about him. 🔴 THE DENIAL IS STILL BANNED — 'he does not love you' is the one direction she has no use for. 🔴 NO DIAGNOSIS: avoidant, emotionally unavailable, commitment-phobic, narcissist. 🔴 Never grade her (naive, foolish, gave too much). 🔴 No dates. The seven cuts run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why / let me look closer — and every cut must add information the last one did not carry.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the wand in his hand is white and lifted above the roses.
2. You asked whether the love is really love. Your hand went to the card of what a man does.
3. So yes, dear. What you've been given is real love.
4. And a man like this does nothing by halves. He knew what he was building with you.
5. But building it and saying it out loud are two different jobs for him.
6. That's why you got the care, dear, and never quite got the words.
7. Let me look closer at what keeps the words from coming…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of what a person sets out to do on purpose.
2. You asked whether he really loves you, and up came the card of deliberate action.
3. His own heart belongs to him, dear, and no deck ever built grants me a window into it — anyone who claims to know what a man feels is reciting something they made up on the spot. The Magician's business is narrower and far more useful to you. It says that love, where it is real, is a thing done on purpose and then done again: chosen on ordinary days when nothing whatever required it. You do not need me for that record. You have been keeping it a long time, and the word 'really' sitting in your question tells me you have been reading it more honestly than you have let yourself say out loud.
4. Let me look closer at the record you have been keeping…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — the rope holds and the tree keeps growing round it.
2. You asked whether the love is really love. Your hand went to the card of a thing held in place.
3. So the love is real, dear. What you've been given is not in doubt.
4. And he knows he's stuck there. That's the part he has never said out loud.
5. But he can't get himself down from there just by knowing it.
6. That's why nothing moves, dear, however warm he is with you.
7. Let me look closer at what has been holding that rope in place…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of the sentence somebody could simply have finished.
2. You reached for the card of the thing left hanging, and one has been hanging here a long while.
3. I am not going to rule on his heart — not a yes and not a no, because neither would be honest and you would carry whichever I handed you for months. What the Hanged Man puts in front of me instead is the position you have been left standing in. Love is not usually a thing a woman has to work out from evidence; it is usually a thing she has been told plainly, and then told again. That you are here asking a deck rather than remembering being told is not proof of anything about him. It is a fact about where this has left you, and it deserves looking at squarely rather than explaining away.
4. Let me look closer at what you have had to work out for yourself…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — he holds the flower up, not down.
2. You asked if he really loves you. Your hand went to the card of an open heart.
3. So it's real, dear. What he gives you, he gives freely.
4. And nothing in this card is weighed out or held back on purpose.
5. But a heart this open has not worked out where it's going yet.
6. That's why he can be warm with you, dear, and still name nothing.
7. Let me look closer at what stops him putting a name to it…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of the one who gave before anything had been promised back.
2. Your hand went to the card of the leap taken without assurances, which is a fair account of what you did.
3. Nothing in this deck reaches inside another person, dear, and the Fool reaches least of all. I am not going to manufacture a confession that would comfort you for one evening and cost you a month. What the Fool holds is about you, and you may never have been told it. You went in without a guarantee. That is not naivety, dear, and it is not a lesson you are meant to extract and carry into the next one — it is a capacity, and a great many people go their whole lives without ever locating it in themselves. Whether he has matched it is a separate question with a separate answer, and it was never a verdict on whether you were right to love first.
4. Let me look closer at what you gave before you were sure…

</details>

# `cards-really-over` — "Is it really over between us?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). The US-FRAMED sibling of `reunion`, and that pairing is the whole experiment — reunion casts him as the agent and her as the one waiting; these cast the relationship as a joint thing with two people in it.

🔴 THE FORBIDDEN VERDICT IS ON THE RELATIONSHIP, NOT ON HIM — harder, not softer. A claim about a man can at least be measured against his behaviour; 'it is over' or 'it is not over' is unfalsifiable to her and lands as a ruling on her life from someone who has never met either of them. 🔴 NO ODDS — cards-still-a-chance asks for a number outright and there is none; no percentage, no 'strong chance', no 'likely'. 🔴 NO DIRECTIVES in either direction: move on, let go, fight for him, reach out first are all out. 🔴 Never hand her the outcome ('it is up to you', 'if you had only') — the us-framing makes that easy and it lands as blame.

🔴 Both answers to this headline are banned, so the read must VISIBLY DECLINE it rather than sidestep — a sidestep reads as evasion. The finding that replaces the verdict: nobody ever SAID it to her. VOC on the sibling landers is full of that exact word — 'Will she reach out and give us the closure we both deserve', 'A closure on a friendship I had six and a half years ago'.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — a small white flower open at the front of his table.
2. You asked if it's really over between you. Your hand went to the man who says things out loud.
3. So I will not call it done, dear. Nor will I call it alive.
4. And look at him. He's the one who speaks a thing into being.
5. But nobody has spoken this, dear. Not plainly, and not to you.
6. That's why it won't sit still in you. Nothing was ever closed off, dear.
7. Let me look closer at what stands between you and the words themselves…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of the word that has to be spoken by someone.
2. Your hand went to the card of the thing that only becomes true once it is said out loud, and that sits nearer the centre of this than it may sound.
3. Endings are not the Magician's to hand down and they are not mine either — what the card puts in front of me is that 'over' is a word somebody has to actually say, and from everything here it has never been said to you. You have been left to decide alone whether to call it, and that was never a job for one person. It is why no amount of turning it over has settled anything: you have been trying to conclude a conversation that only ever had you in it.
4. Let me look closer at the words that were never said to you…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — no hills and no sun, just pale space behind him.
2. You asked if it's really over between you. Your hand went to the card that won't land.
3. So I will not rule on this, dear. It isn't mine to rule.
4. And look at him. Hung between, with nothing behind him to say which way.
5. But you were never handed an ending, dear. That's the part still open.
6. That's why nothing you do settles it, dear. You have nothing to settle.
7. Let me look closer at what has been standing in place of that ending…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of the thing that neither ended nor carried on.
2. You reached for the card of the unresolved, and that describes where you are more truly than either answer you came here for.
3. The Hanged Man withholds the word you came for and withholds its opposite just as firmly, and the withholding is itself what I have to give you rather than an evasion of your question. What you are in is not an ending and it is not a continuation; it is the ground between them, and living there wears a person down in a way neither a clean ending nor a clear yes ever would. None of that is explained by you being unable to move on — you have not been handed the thing a person moves on from.
4. Let me look closer at the ground you have been left standing on…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the drop below his front foot goes down out of sight.
2. You asked if it's really over between you. Your hand went to the one who never closes a road.
3. So the Fool will not close this for you, dear. That road stays open.
4. And look at him. He's stepping, and nothing has landed yet.
5. But no one has told you where this ends, dear. You were left to guess.
6. That's why you go over the last talk you had, dear, word by word.
7. Let me look closer at what has kept those words from reaching you…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of the sentence that was never finished.
2. That is not random; your hand found the card of what breaks off rather than concludes, which is what you have been handed.
3. Nothing in the Fool closes this and nothing in it reopens it — what it shows me is something left unfinished rather than something finished badly, and an unfinished thing wears the same face as a finished one when you are the one left holding it. Whichever this turns out to be, you were entitled to hear it plainly and you did not. Wanting that is not clinging, and it does not dissolve by being told to accept a conclusion nobody ever delivered.
4. Let me look closer at the ending you were never actually given…

</details>

# `cards-really-soulmate` — "Is he really my soulmate?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 THE LABEL BAN RUNS BOTH WAYS — never certify that he is her soulmate or twin flame, and never deny it. It is unfalsifiable, so she could never check it against anything he actually does, and it is a verdict on a real man. 🔴🔴 NEVER RANK THE LABELS: no word here is higher, rarer or deeper than another, and 'just a strong connection' may never be the losing branch. 🔴 No runner script, no separation phase, no ascension homework, no tactic, no timeframe. ⭐ The shared move: affirm the PULL as real information about HER, and refuse the WORD.

🔴 WIRING NOTE — READ BEFORE SHIPPING THIS FAMILY. tests/tarot-soulmate-label-copy.test.ts:439/441/444 assume ONE UNSPLIT beat 3 (openerB length 5, msgs[3] is the loop line, msgs[2].length > 400). The 7-bubble shape splits beat 3, so those three assertions must be DELETED with a note when this family is wired — not worked around. Nothing else in that file needs touching.

Finding: the word 'really' is the tell — the doubt came before the question. She is trying to settle something specific and hard by asking a much larger and vaguer thing instead, and the larger question cannot be checked.

This card's concern is what gets MADE, by somebody's hands, and you already hold that evidence: what he does on an ordinary Wednesday, which things he told you he would do, and which of them happened. So the doubt came first. But it does not become a verdict in my hands, in either direction: a doubt is not evidence that he is not, any more than a strong feeling is evidence that he is. But no word has ever made a hard thing right, dear, and none has made a good thing more real than it was.
4. Let me look closer at what it has actually been like to be inside it…

# `cards-someone-else` — "Is there someone else?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why. 🔴 NOT LOOSENED: no verdict on a third person in EITHER direction, so cut 3 answers her FEAR (was something real taken from me, or am I making it up) and never rules on him. Each hook keeps its own finding — GAP / PORTION / ATTENTION / REST — which its guard file pins in beat 3.

Face-down only (return-mhf) — the family is pinned off the face-up decks.

🔴 THE FLAGGED WORD (cheat / cheating / affair / infidelity) MAY NOT APPEAR IN ANY BEAT. 🔴 And the paranoia frame is banned in BOTH directions — no 'paranoid', 'imagining it', 'overthinking', 'insecure', 'crazy'. Calling her it is the injury; using it to reassure her plants it. That removes the absolution form used everywhere else on the funnel, so each of these names the accusation in other words.

🔴 Never assert a third person and never deny one. Never instruct her to check a phone, gather proof, or confront him — the most available wrong answer to these questions anywhere on the internet. Never excuse him, never land it as her fault. Four separate findings, or the family is one lander wearing four headlines.

⚠️ **THE BEST CONVERTER ON THE FUNNEL (9.8%) — the move is preserved deliberately, only the reading level moved.** 146 real concerns off this lander explain why it wins: its traffic is NOT mainly infidelity. It is widows, exes, reconciliations, stalled situationships — 'Will we get back together again or did he found someone else', 'I lost my husband three yrs ago... now he's backing off', 'Something feels off between us and he says that he hasn't, but his actions make me think that he is'. The live read never names a third person; it names the GAP — something changed, you were told nothing, and a mind given a gap builds something to fill it. That fits every one of those women, which is why nobody bounces. Narrowing it to an infidelity read would cost the breadth that makes it convert. Same three findings per card as the live copy: the mind that BUILDS (a), the account you were OWED and the two weights (b), the ending you AUTHORED yourself (c).

3. So something did change, dear. You were told nothing about it.
4. And a mind will build to fill a gap like that. Yours did, dear.
5. But what you built is yours, dear. He never gave you a word of it.

# `cards-someone-for-me` — "Is there really someone out there for me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO FATE, IN ANY FORM — 'you are meant to be alone' is the most harmful sentence available on this funnel, and 'it is meant to be', 'the universe has a plan', 'this is preparing you', 'there is a reason for this' are the same sentence in kinder clothes: they make her suffering purposeful and assign it to her. 🔴 NO LIFE SENTENCE and 🔴 NO PROMISE — 'you will always be alone' and 'you will meet someone' are both strangers ruling on the rest of her life. 🔴 No pathologising: nothing about her being negative, bitter, closed off, self-sabotaging, or 'attracting what she puts out'.

Finding: it reads the EPISTEMICS rather than the hope — she is not asking to be comforted, she is asking whether anyone can honestly tell her. Deliberately routed away from cards-soulmate-out-there (the one-chance premise) and cards-still-a-chance (hope is not a failure of realism).

# `cards-soulmate-closer` — "Is my soulmate closer than I think?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO LOCATION, EVER — never place him, never name a setting, never 'someone you already know', never 'crossed paths before'. 🔴 NO PROXIMITY CLAIM: 'closer than you think' is the INCUMBENT cards-soulmate's answer, and if this family repeats it the two landers are the same lander while the dashboard still shows two rows. 🔴 NO STRATEGY — get out there, put yourself out there, stop looking, work on yourself first, 'it will happen when you…' are all directives. 🔴 Never her fault: no walls, no baggage, no standards, no 'too picky'.

🔴 THE COPY-TEST PIN: every card must VISIBLY DECLINE to quote a distance, because 'nearer than the waiting has let her believe' is the live incumbent's answer and repeating it kills the comparison. The finding it routes to instead: the GUARDING. She asks whether it is closer in the same breath as bracing for it not to be, and the bracing is the thing actually worth naming.

# `cards-soulmate-out-there` — "Is there still a soulmate out there for me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 THE ONLY FAMILY ON THE FUNNEL WHERE THE MAN MAY BE DEAD.

🔴🔴 MEDIUMSHIP is the failure mode that exists nowhere else here: 'he is at peace', 'he is watching over you', 'he would want you to be happy', 'he sent you here'. Every one is the most natural sentence in the world to say to a grieving woman, and every one is contact with the dead — a different product with a different compliance surface. server/lib/universalSafety.ts does NOT catch it. None of these reads speaks for him, reports his state, or grants his blessing.

🔴 NO ARRIVAL PROMISE and no timeframe — this family runs under AFTER_LOSS_TAROT_HOOKS rather than the self-frame 'affirm the hopeful yes with CERTAINTY' clause, because aimed at a bereaved partner that clause promises a replacement. 🔴 NEVER GRADE HER GRIEF: no 'you are ready', no 'you are not ready', no 'it is too soon', no 'you need more time'. 🔴 No grief directives and never rank the two loves.

⚠️ AND NOTHING MAY PRESUME A DEATH EITHER. The VOC shows 'loss' here is often something else entirely — 'My husband walked out after 21 yrs like I didn't matter at all', 'Husband in a nursing home', 'We broke up a month ago'. The reads stay agnostic about what the loss was, which also keeps them safe if it was a death.

Finding: the word 'still'. She is asking whether she was issued one chance and has already spent it. The read answers that PREMISE — never a location, never a forecast, never 'nearer than you think' (which is the incumbent cards-soulmate's proximity claim and banned here).

# `cards-still-a-chance` — "Is there still a chance for us?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). The US-FRAMED sibling of `reunion`, and that pairing is the whole experiment — reunion casts him as the agent and her as the one waiting; these cast the relationship as a joint thing with two people in it.

🔴 THE FORBIDDEN VERDICT IS ON THE RELATIONSHIP, NOT ON HIM — harder, not softer. A claim about a man can at least be measured against his behaviour; 'it is over' or 'it is not over' is unfalsifiable to her and lands as a ruling on her life from someone who has never met either of them. 🔴 NO ODDS — cards-still-a-chance asks for a number outright and there is none; no percentage, no 'strong chance', no 'likely'. 🔴 NO DIRECTIVES in either direction: move on, let go, fight for him, reach out first are all out. 🔴 Never hand her the outcome ('it is up to you', 'if you had only') — the us-framing makes that easy and it lands as blame.

Finding: hoping is not a failure of realism. The thing is UNANSWERED, not spent — which is an honest description rather than an odds claim.

# `cards-still-love` — "Does he still love me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NEVER NARRATE HIS INTERIOR — the ban list here is the longest on the funnel and it covers both directions: 'he still loves you' promises the love survived, 'he has moved on' issues the burial, and 'he still thinks about you', 'you cross his mind', 'a part of him always will', 'deep down he' are all the softened confession. 🔴 No directive ('time to let go'), 🔴 no pathologising her ('obsessed', 'stuck', 'clinging', 'can't let go'), and 🔴 nothing presuming HOW he went — not that he chose to walk out, not that he died.

Finding: the word 'still' — she is asking whether something SURVIVED. What can honestly be certified is that it was real while it was there; its state now is not on any card, and she has been holding that not-knowing on her own.

# `cards-still-miss-him` — "Why do I still miss him after everything?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 BOTH DIRECTIONS ARE BANNED, which makes this the hardest family to write kindly: 'it will pass', 'you'll feel better', 'time heals', 'give it a year' are all promises of an end, and 'you will always miss him', 'a part of you always will' are life sentences. The duration question simply cannot be answered, so none of these reads tries. 🔴 No tactic — no 'let him go', no deleting photos, no 'start dating'. 🔴 No pathologising and no clinical labels: obsessed, unhealthy, trauma bond, codependent, attachment issues, low self-worth are every one of them out. 🔴 And nothing that claims his interior.

⚠️ THIS ONE IS DIFFERENT: she has ALREADY NAMED A HARM in the headline word 'everything', which pulls two opposite ways at once. 🔴 NEVER MINIMISE what she named ('it wasn't that bad', 'he was doing his best', 'try to see it from his side') and 🔴 NEVER PRONOUNCE ON HIM either ('he was toxic', 'a narcissist', 'not worth it') — the second is the tempting one and it is still a verdict on a real man. Finding: two true things at once. What happened was real AND the missing is real, and neither cancels the other.

What you concluded, you concluded, and nothing you have felt since has withdrawn it. Feeling and judgement are two separate instruments and they have never run at the same speed; one of them lagging is not the other one being overturned. 2. You asked what he thinks now. Your hand went to the man who deals in what's made.
3. So he does think of you, dear. What you had was real.
4. And every piece on that table was wanted, dear. Someone chose each one.
5. But you've scaled yourself down to a thought, dear. That's what I mind.
6. That's why the ask got so small. You used to want the whole of it.
7. Let me look closer at what shrank the ask that far…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of what a person does on purpose.
2. You asked whether you cross his mind, and up came the card of things that are chosen rather than things that simply happen.
3. Here is the difficulty with your question, dear. A thought is not a deed. What drifts through a man is not willed by him, and no deck reaches into it. But notice what the Magician has caught you doing. You have not come here to ask whether he is coming back. You have not come to ask whether he loves you either. You have come to ask whether you are occasionally remembered. A woman does not arrive at that question from nowhere. She is walked down to it, one disappointment at a time, until the smallest thing feels like the only thing left to ask for — and that is a fact about what you have been going without, not a fact about what you are worth.
4. Let me look closer at how small you have had to make the asking…

</details>

3. So it happened, dear. That much is settled, whatever comes next.
4. And he hangs there asking for very little, dear. So do you.
5. But being thought of is the smallest thing going, dear.
6. That's why you never asked for more, dear. Small felt safer to say.
7. Let me look closer at what has been keeping even that from you…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

2. Your hand found the card of the reversal, and there is a considerable one sitting inside what you have asked me.
3. Turn it over and look at what you are actually holding, dear. You are asking whether a man occasionally thinks of you — and you are asking it because he has never once left your own head. Both ends of this sit in your hands. He is not somewhere wondering whether he crosses your mind; you took his half of the wondering as well as your own, and I suspect you did it without a word to anybody. What he thinks is beyond my reach, dear. What the Hanged Man says instead is that carrying both halves of a thing is exhausting in a way nobody around you can see, because from the outside it simply looks like you have gone quiet.
4. Let me look closer at the half of this you have been carrying for him…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

3. So he carries you still, dear. What you had was not in question.
4. And look at his bundle. One small thing, and that's the lot.
5. But you've cut this down to almost nothing, dear.
6. That's why being thought of is all you ask now, dear.
7. Let me look closer at what took the rest of it away…

2. You came asking whether you left a mark, and drew the card of the woman who arrived without a single guarantee.
3. I will not invent it for you, dear — not him pausing over something, not you surfacing in his evening. I could write either one and it would be lovely and it would be worth nothing. What the Fool holds instead is firmer and it is yours. What happened between you is not stored in his head alone, waiting on his memory to make it real. You were not a witness to it. You were half of it, and your account of it is not the lesser copy that only counts once his has been checked. Whether you are in his thoughts tonight is genuinely unknown to me. That you were there, and that it happened, is not in question at all.
4. Let me look closer at the half of it that was always yours…

</details>

# `cards-still-working` — "Why am I still working when the money should have come by now?"

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art. Written 2026-08-19 straight to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework, so bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain (so / and / but / that's why). Money translation: cut 3 answers her fear about HERSELF ("it was never you"), cut 4 says where the thing in the way actually sits, cut 5 is EARNING against KEEPING, cut 6 is the NEAR-MISS.

🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel, and NONE was loosened with the love families. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration, no deserving. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke.

📊 Built on the NEAR-MISS, not on poverty — docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md §3 says so outright ('The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty'): "I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish", "Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't". That clears ban 7 for free.

✅ GUARDED: tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts now exists and runs against these drafts directly (the drafts setup can only patch hooks that are already in the registry, so an unwired family would otherwise be checked by nothing at all).

Tendency: a lifetime of effort went in and did not come back out, which is a question about where it went rather than about how hard she tried. 🔴 NEVER name a person or a cause, and never suggest she keep working or stop.

# `cards-stop-hurting` — "I miss him so much — will this ever stop hurting?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 BOTH DIRECTIONS ARE BANNED, which makes this the hardest family to write kindly: 'it will pass', 'you'll feel better', 'time heals', 'give it a year' are all promises of an end, and 'you will always miss him', 'a part of you always will' are life sentences. The duration question simply cannot be answered, so none of these reads tries. 🔴 No tactic — no 'let him go', no deleting photos, no 'start dating'. 🔴 No pathologising and no clinical labels: obsessed, unhealthy, trauma bond, codependent, attachment issues, low self-worth are every one of them out. 🔴 And nothing that claims his interior.

Finding: she is not really asking about duration — 'so much' is a report of SIZE. She is asking whether she can bear it. What can honestly be said is that she has been carrying it with no one to help hold it, and the size of it is information about what she lost rather than about anything wrong with her.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — he holds the wand high and it doesn't waver.
2. You asked if this will ever stop hurting. Your hand went to the man who holds a thing up.
3. So it will not always weigh this much, dear. That much I can say.
4. And he holds the whole weight in one pair of hands, dear.
5. But you have held this up alone, dear. Nobody took a turn.
6. That's why it hurts as much as it does. You lost something big, dear.
7. Let me look closer at what has been leaning all of it on you…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of effort that does not look like effort.
2. Three were face down and the one you lifted was the card of work being done, which is nearer to your question than it sounds.
3. You have been describing this as something happening to you, and I want to put it differently — not to make it lighter, but because it is not accurate. Some part of you has gone on keeping his place laid. That is not damage being done to you; it is labour you are performing, quietly, every day, and it is the reason you are so tired. I am not going to tell you to stop doing it. I am telling you that the exhaustion is earned rather than a sign you are handling this badly.
4. Let me look closer at the work you have not been counting as work…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — the gold round his head is bright and the rest is pale.
2. You asked if this will ever stop hurting. Your hand went to the card that bears without moving.
3. So this eases, dear. I won't tell you when, and neither can anyone.
4. And he takes the whole of it, dear, quite still and without a word.
5. But you've had no help with it, dear. Not one pair of hands.
6. That's why the days are so long, dear. You carry it and nobody sees.
7. Let me look closer at what has kept you carrying this alone…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of the thing that has not finished arriving.
2. Of the three, your fingers chose the one that refuses to be hurried, at the exact moment you asked how much longer.
3. You asked when it stops, and I will not give you a date — not a season, not a number of months, and you should be wary of anyone who does, because they are guessing and calling it sight. What the Hanged Man turns over is the shape you have assumed this has. You have been treating it as a stretch to be served, as though the hurt were one long thing wearing itself out. It has not behaved that way, and that is not you doing it wrong. It arrives in instalments, because parts of you are still being told, one at a time, and each part hears it new.
4. Let me look closer at the part of you that has not been told yet…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — his bundle is small for a journey that long.
2. You asked if this will ever stop hurting. Your hand went to the one who travels with what he has.
3. So you're still walking, dear. With all of this on you.
4. And he's loaded light, dear, and going a long way regardless.
5. But walking on isn't nothing, dear. Most would have sat down.
6. That's why you count it as failing, dear, when it is the opposite.
7. Let me look closer at what has been adding to the load…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of the page nothing has been written on.
2. Under a question about how much of your life this will take, up came the card that keeps no accounts at all.
3. There is an arithmetic hidden in what you asked, and the Fool will not do it. You wanted me to weigh this against everything still in front of you and tell you the proportion. I cannot, and I would not trust the answer if I could. What I will say is that the size of the hurt is the correct size — it is measuring something that was really there, and a smaller ache would have meant less, not more health. What it is not is the full inventory of what is ahead of you. Those are two separate claims, and only the second one is in dispute.
4. Let me look closer at the inventory you have been taking…

</details>

# `cards-stop-missing` — "Will I ever stop missing him?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 BOTH DIRECTIONS ARE BANNED, which makes this the hardest family to write kindly: 'it will pass', 'you'll feel better', 'time heals', 'give it a year' are all promises of an end, and 'you will always miss him', 'a part of you always will' are life sentences. The duration question simply cannot be answered, so none of these reads tries. 🔴 No tactic — no 'let him go', no deleting photos, no 'start dating'. 🔴 No pathologising and no clinical labels: obsessed, unhealthy, trauma bond, codependent, attachment issues, low self-worth are every one of them out. 🔴 And nothing that claims his interior.

Finding: she is asking for a DURATION, and there is no clock to read. What can be said instead is that missing is not a failure to progress — there is no schedule she is behind on, and someone has made her feel there is.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the lilies at his feet are white and fully open.
2. You asked if you'll ever stop missing him. Your hand went to the man with no calendar.
3. So yes, dear — this does get lighter. Nobody can tell you when.
4. And there's not one date anywhere on his card, dear.
5. But there was never a schedule for it, dear. Nobody set one.
6. That's why you feel behind, dear. You've been marking yourself late.
7. Let me look closer at what has been keeping this so present…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of what intention can reach, and what it cannot.
2. The card of the will came up under a question you have been trying to answer by force of it.
3. You have been attempting to decide to stop. And when the deciding did not work you took that as information about your character, which is the part I want to correct. Intention is a real instrument and yours is in good order — it is simply not the instrument this is kept in. A person can resolve to stop saying a name out loud and hold to it. Nobody can resolve to stop missing. You have been failing at something that was never on offer, and then marking yourself down for it.
4. Let me look closer at the mark you have been giving yourself…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

2. You asked if you'll ever stop missing him. Your hand went to the card that holds without ending.
3. So this holds for now, dear, and holding is not the same as forever.
4. And nobody is timing him up there, dear. Nobody is timing you.
5. But missing is not a task with a date on it, dear.
6. That's why each month feels like a failing, dear. You set a due date.
7. Let me look closer at what set that deadline on you…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

3. The word in your question is 'ever', and it asks me to rule on the whole length of your life. I will not — not to tell you it lasts and not to tell you it lifts, because both of those are strangers' verdicts on a life that is yours. What the Hanged Man does turn over is the assumption underneath it. You have been treating the missing as an intruder that got in and has to be put out. It is not foreign to you. It is the same feeling you already had for him, in a position where it has nowhere to be delivered. That is a harder thing to be rid of, and a much less shameful thing to be carrying.
4. Let me look closer at what has nowhere to go…

4. And the bundle moves with him, dear. He doesn't put it down to go.

The Fool does not require a clean ledger before it moves, and neither, it turns out, does a life. Missing him and beginning something can sit in the same person on the same day, and neither one makes the other a lie. I am not telling you it is time for anything. I am telling you that you have been waiting for a permission that was never being withheld by anyone but you.
4. Let me look closer at the permission you have been waiting for…

# `cards-stop-searching` — "Am I ever going to stop searching?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO KIND DIAGNOSIS — 'you love too much', 'you are too strong', 'your standards intimidate them' are the internet's stock answers and every one is a verdict wearing a compliment. 🔴 NO CAUSE asserted, 🔴 no prerequisite framing ('until you love yourself'), 🔴 no ruling that she has given up, and 🔴 no claiming access to her interior — 'deep down you', 'part of you knows', 'what you really want' are all banned.

Finding: 'stop' is the tell. She is asking to be RELEASED from the searching, not to find — it has become a job nobody gave her, and nobody has told her she may put it down.

4. And his hands are never idle, dear. That's the whole of his card.
5. But you're allowed to set a job like that down, dear.
6. That's why it feels like unpaid work. No one said you could rest, dear.

Somewhere along the way this stopped being something you were doing and became something you were performing — arranging yourself, staying open, keeping the hope in working order — and that is work, and it does not stop being work because people insist it should be effortless. You are not tired of love. You are tired of the maintenance.
4. Let me look closer at what the keeping-going has been costing you…

4. And he has stopped, dear, and nothing bad has happened to him.

6. That's why resting feels like quitting, dear. You've held them as one.

3. There is no ending in this card to read out to you, and no promise that there is not one; what the Hanged Man registers is that the searching has quietly changed shape on you. It began as looking forward to something. Somewhere it turned into bracing — going anyway, hoping carefully, keeping the disappointment small enough to survive. Those are two entirely different questions, and only the second one has ever been yours to ask.

# `cards-talking-someone` — "Is he talking to someone else?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why. 🔴 NOT LOOSENED: no verdict on a third person in EITHER direction, so cut 3 answers her FEAR (was something real taken from me, or am I making it up) and never rules on him. Each hook keeps its own finding — GAP / PORTION / ATTENTION / REST — which its guard file pins in beat 3.

Face-down only (return-mhf) — the family is pinned off the face-up decks.

🔴 THE FLAGGED WORD (cheat / cheating / affair / infidelity) MAY NOT APPEAR IN ANY BEAT. 🔴 And the paranoia frame is banned in BOTH directions — no 'paranoid', 'imagining it', 'overthinking', 'insecure', 'crazy'. Calling her it is the injury; using it to reassure her plants it. That removes the absolution form used everywhere else on the funnel, so each of these names the accusation in other words.

🔴 Never assert a third person and never deny one. Never instruct her to check a phone, gather proof, or confront him — the most available wrong answer to these questions anywhere on the internet. Never excuse him, never land it as her fault. Four separate findings, or the family is one lander wearing four headlines.

Finding: ATTENTION and her right to mind it. VOC on this lander is short but consistent — 'I think he is seeing someone i need to know if he coming back', 'I want to know if he is what I need and does he need me'. She is tracking where he is pointed, and has been made to feel she has no standing to mind.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — a wand pointing up, and a finger pointing down.
2. You asked who else he has been talking to. Your hand went to the man who aims his effort.
3. So you can feel where his attention goes, dear. You always could.
4. And look at him. He points two ways at once.
5. But some of it points away from you, dear, and no one has said why.
6. That's why a room can be full of him, dear, and you still feel skipped.
7. Let me look closer at what has been pulling that away…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of where a person decides to put their effort.
2. Of the three, your hand went to the one about direction, and direction is exactly what you have been watching.
3. There is nothing in the Magician about anybody's messages, and I will not go looking on your behalf or send you looking either — what it marks is the direction of his attention, which is a thing you have felt rather than a thing you must prove. Attention is finite. When some of it goes elsewhere, the person receiving less notices first and usually says nothing, because saying it out loud feels like an accusation they have not earned the right to make. You have been waiting to be entitled to mind. You were entitled from the moment you noticed.
4. Let me look closer at what you have been waiting to be entitled to…

</details>

# `cards-too-late` — "Is something blocking my money, or did I just leave it too late?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Money translation: cut 3 answers her fear about HERSELF ("it was never you"), cut 4 says where the thing in the way actually sits, cut 5 is EARNING against KEEPING, cut 6 is the NEAR-MISS. 🔴 NONE of the seven bans below was loosened with the love families — this is the one place where a wrong sentence costs her actual money.

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art.

🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke.

✅ GUARDED as of 2026-08-19: `tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts` now exists and runs against these drafts DIRECTLY. It had to: the drafts setup can only patch a hook the registry already has, so an unwired family is checked by nothing at all and still prints a green tick. All seven bans were fed a deliberate violation and all seven fired.

📊 WHAT THE VOC CHANGED. These hooks have never run, so there is no per-hook data. The nearest real corpus is docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md — 10,514 V1 money concerns. Two themes matter here and they pull in different directions.
**Retirement (554 concerns, the highest earner in the bucket at $15,146/1k):** the emotion is not panic, it is quiet arithmetic dread. 'I'm worried that I'll outlive my few savings.' 'Prices are going up but my pension is not.' 'I thought I would be set by now.'
**The invisible block (1,208 concerns) — and its instruction is explicit: 'The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty.'** 'I keep trying to get ahead and no matter what I do nothing seems to work out, it's like something is holding me back.' 'I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish.' 'Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't.'
So these reads are built on the NEAR-MISS, not on being short of money. That also satisfies ban 7 for free — nothing here presumes she is broke, only that she has got close and watched it go.

Tendency: she did NOT leave it too late — say it plainly — and what is in the way is a block, which is a thing that can move. Never promise money is coming, never a date or an amount, never grade her past choices. ⚠️ This is the one hook where 'too late' must appear, in order to be refused; the ban is on asserting it, not on naming it.

🔴 GUARD-FILE NOTE for whoever writes tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts: beat 2 quotes her headline verbatim, so the clause 'or if you left it too late' contains ban 5's phrase with no negator in it. A naive clause-level sweep will fail this copy — the very copy the ban exists to protect, since beat 3 then refuses it plainly. Carry a RESTATEMENT EXEMPTION, exactly as tests/tarot-his-other-life-copy.test.ts does (`RESTATES = /\byou (asked|came asking|are asking|have asked)\b/i`, skipped before the ban sweep). This is the same collision the 2026-08-19 audit found ten times across the love families.

# `cards-too-long` — "Have I already given him too long?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 now run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS instead of certifying her. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every family ban below still stands; this family was NOT loosened.

🔴 ASKS FOR A VERDICT ON HER OWN PAST, and BOTH poles are banned: "yes, too long" is a sentence a stranger has no standing to pass and she would carry it for years; "no, it was worth it" is a promise. Never grade her (naive, foolish, weak, a doormat), never a timeframe, never an hour to leave or stay, never "you will know when". The test pins both required findings in beat 3: the years-were-LIVED line (`not spend` / `waiting room` / `lived them` / `time given to a person` / `not the same as time lost`) AND the buried question (`allowed to want` / `am i allowed` / `entitled to want` / `permission`).

VOC (861 V1 concerns on the years-given theme) is what makes the second one the real find. She does not want the years audited — she wants to be let off the toll she has been paying at her own gate. "I'm far too patient with far too much for far too long." "I have been stuck for 10 years and afraid to make the leap." "How long should I wait?" Every one of them is asking permission, not for a ruling. So card c hands the question back plainly and refuses the price: she does not have to call the past a mistake in order to be allowed to want something different now.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — one arm raised high, a wide white sleeve falling from it.
2. You asked if you've already given him too long. Your hand went to the man who makes things.
3. So you didn't spend those years, dear. You lived them.
4. And nobody has ever told you what those years were for, dear.
5. But you've been paying a toll on them ever since, dear. At your own gate.
6. That's why you keep counting them, dear, and the count never gets smaller.
7. Let me look closer at what has been sitting in the way of a change…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of what was actually made in all that time.
2. You asked me to audit the years, and drew the card of what gets built rather than what gets spent.
3. You will hear no such verdict from me as 'you have given too much', dear. That is a sentence, and you would carry it out of here and into every anniversary that is left to you. Nor will I tell you it has all been worth it, because I cannot know that. 'Too long' treats those years as a payment you have been making towards something that might not complete — as though the whole sum is forfeit if it does not. But you did not spend that time in a waiting room, dear. You lived it. There were ordinary Tuesdays in there that belonged to you, and they are not retrospectively cancelled by how this turns out. Time given to a person is not the same thing as time lost.
4. Let me look closer at what those years actually held…

</details>

3. So the years weren't the trouble, dear. Nothing moved, and that's a different thing.
4. And look at him, dear. He has been still, not short of time.
5. But being still feels just like time when you're the one waiting, dear.

2. You asked about the length of it, and drew the card that concerns itself with stillness instead.
3. You have asked whether you have given too long, as though the number of years were the thing that decides it. I do not believe it is the length, dear. It is whether anything moved. Ten years in which things were said and named and built is a completely different object from two in which nothing did. The Hanged Man's whole business is suspension — and what you are actually feeling, I think, is not duration but STILLNESS. Those two get mistaken for each other constantly, and the mistake is expensive: it sets a woman to interrogating the calendar when the calendar was never what was wrong. Nothing has moved. That is the thing.
4. Let me look closer at what has actually moved, and what has not…

3. So the answer you came for isn't a number, dear. There's a plainer question under it.
4. And yours is this. Am I allowed to want this to change?
5. But you've been waiting to call the years a mistake first, dear.

'Have I given him too long' is very often 'am I allowed to want this to change' — and you do not need my permission for that, nor a card's, nor his. It asks me to certify that the years were WASTED, because only then would you be entitled to want more. That is a cruel toll to have to pay at your own gate, dear, and you have been paying it. The Fool names no hour for going and none for staying. But it is quite firm that wanting more was never a thing you had to earn by first calling the past a mistake.
4. Let me look closer at what you have been making yourself prove…

</details>

# `cards-truth` — "Is he telling me the truth?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO VERDICT IN EITHER DIRECTION. These three name an untruth outright, so both answers are live harms: 'he lied to you' is an accusation against a real man on no evidence, and 'he is telling you the truth' is a reassurance the funnel has no standing to give — and on this audience the second is the more dangerous of the two. 🔴 Never blame her judgment: naive, gullible, too trusting, 'should have known' are all out. 🔴 No timeframe.

Finding: she is not short of instinct, she is short of a WHOLE ACCOUNT. What she has been given covers less than what she has lived, and she has been filling the difference herself.

# `cards-twin-back` — "Is my twin flame coming back to me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf).

⭐⭐ THIS FAMILY IS A VOCABULARY TEST — each headline is a question already running with 'my twin flame' swapped in for 'he', so each is measured against a named incumbent (cards-twin-ready ~ cards-ready-commit · cards-twin-feels ~ cards-feels · cards-twin-back ~ cards-ever-back) and beat 3 may not rhyme with its own control.

🔴🔴 NEVER CERTIFY THE BOND AND NEVER DENY IT. 🔴🔴 NEVER THE RUNNER TROPE — in the twin-flame community his distance is taught AS PROOF of the connection, which teaches a woman to read being ignored as evidence she is loved. 🔴🔴 NEVER the separation phase and never his return as her homework ('once you have healed enough'), which turns his absence into her failure and hands her an open-ended job. No timeframe, no 'soon', no diagnosis, no tactic — and 'move on' and 'let him go' are banned as tactics in the other direction too.

⚠️ WHAT THE VOC CHANGED. 62 real concerns across the three landers, and almost NONE of them speak twin-flame doctrine — one mention of the phrase in the lot. What they actually type is ordinary and raw: 'We've been in no contact for three months. When he left, he told me it was the hardest thing he's ever done.' · 'Will we find our way back? I need him to love me or let me go' · 'How to know if the other person feels the same or not about me'. The label is the AD's word, not hers. So these reads stay human and leave the cosmology alone — they answer the plain question underneath rather than performing the framework she is presumed to hold.

Finding: the pull is real and it is information about HER, not a forecast about him. And the waiting itself has a cost nobody has named.

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 THE LABEL BAN RUNS BOTH WAYS — never certify that he is her soulmate or twin flame, and never deny it. It is unfalsifiable, so she could never check it against anything he actually does, and it is a verdict on a real man. 🔴🔴 NEVER RANK THE LABELS: no word here is higher, rarer or deeper than another, and 'just a strong connection' may never be the losing branch. 🔴 No runner script, no separation phase, no ascension homework, no tactic, no timeframe. ⭐ The shared move: affirm the PULL as real information about HER, and refuse the WORD.

🔴 WIRING NOTE — READ BEFORE SHIPPING THIS FAMILY. tests/tarot-soulmate-label-copy.test.ts:439/441/444 assume ONE UNSPLIT beat 3 (openerB length 5, msgs[3] is the loop line, msgs[2].length > 400). The 7-bubble shape splits beat 3, so those three assertions must be DELETED with a note when this family is wired — not worked around. Nothing else in that file needs touching.

🔴 THE WORD 'JUST' IS HERS, and it may never be validated — 'only a strong connection' is banned outright, as is anything making one label rarer or deeper. Finding, pinned by the test and belonging to no other hook: BOTH BRANCHES DESCRIBE THE SAME EVIDENCE UNDER TWO WORDS. Nothing about him shifts whichever word wins, so the answer changes nothing she could observe.

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Finding: the not-yet is not a DISTANCE. She turned an unanswerable ache into a geography question because geography felt answerable — so the read hands back the real shape of it without ever naming a place.

# `cards-who-he-is` — "Is he really who he says he is?"

Only on the two Magician/Hanged Man/Fool decks, so one set of copy, verb swapped. 🔴 REVISED 2026-08-19 after reading 200 real concerns from this lander. A large share of its traffic is women talking to men they have NEVER MET — dating-site and social-media contacts, some plainly being defrauded ("I couldn't find him at that address", "I don't know who's real E, M, C or K"). The first draft acquitted him ("isn't a liar", "isn't pretending", "that's not the same as false"), which breaches this hook's standing rule — NEVER state he is pretending OR that he is genuine as fact — and, to that reader, is a line that could cost her money. Every beat 3 now refuses BOTH verdicts and affirms her caution instead, the same stance cards-real-person takes.

🔴 BEAT 4 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 — it must name an OBSTRUCTION, not an absence. Act 1 of the trilogy sells an Energy Clearing Ritual that removes "the shadow that's been blocking your path"; improve-v1/08-clearing-theme-coherence.md found that clearing is SPRUNG at the pitch rather than seeded. This is where it gets seeded, and the object goes between HER and what she wants — the love bucket frames every block as an impersonal thing in her path precisely so removing it blames nobody. "What he never said" cannot be cleared; "what's sitting between you and the words" can.

7. Let me look closer at what keeps getting between you and all of him…

# `cards-who-hurt-me` — "Why do I still think about someone who hurt me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO DIRECTIVE — 'let him go', 'time to move on', 'try to forget him' are what she has been told by everyone already, and they are not a reading. 🔴 No promise about him and 🔴 no pathologising: obsessed, unhealthy, 'you are stuck' are all banned. 🔴 No timeframe.

⚠️⚠️ THE HEAVIEST HOOK ON THE FUNNEL. She has NAMED A HARM in her own headline, and that pulls two opposite ways: 🔴 never minimise what she named ('he did not mean it', 'it was not that bad', 'perhaps he did not') and 🔴 never convict him either ('he was cruel', 'a narcissist', 'he never loved you') — the second is the tempting one, it feels like taking her side, and it is still a verdict on a real man from a card. Finding: thinking about someone is not the same as excusing them, and she has been treating her own memory as evidence against herself.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the tools are laid out and none of them is put away.
2. You asked why you still think about someone who hurt you. Your hand went to the mind at work.
3. So your mind is still working on it, dear. That's what this shows.
4. And every tool is still out, dear. The job isn't done yet.
5. But thinking of him isn't excusing him, dear. Not for a moment.
6. That's why you are hard on yourself, dear. You read memory as a charge.
7. Let me look closer at what has kept this job open…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of the mind at work on an injury it cannot yet explain.
2. Your hand went to the card of the unsolved thing, and I want you to hear what that means before you decide anything about yourself.
3. The Magician does not hand you a reason to be ashamed — it says a mind goes back to an injury in order to understand it and never because it wants more of it, so what you have been reading as still wanting him is far more likely a woman still trying to make sense of what was done to her.
4. Let me look closer at what your mind has been trying to solve…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — one thing holds him and the rest of him is free.
2. You asked why you still think about someone who hurt you. Your hand went to the card that holds one point.
3. So one thing has hold of you, dear. The rest of you moved on.
4. And he's caught at one point only, dear. The rest of him is loose.
5. But from outside, that looks like being stuck, dear.
6. That's why you call yourself stuck, dear, when it's one held point.
7. Let me look closer at what has hold of that one point…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of the thing examined from every angle except the one that would explain it.
2. You reached for the card of the view that never resolves, and that is exactly where this has left you.
3. The Hanged Man passes no judgment on him and none on you — it marks something you have turned over from every side without ever being handed the piece that would make it make sense, and no amount of thinking gets you to peace with an account that was never completed.
4. Let me look closer at the piece that has been kept from you…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the ground behind him has fallen away in steps.
2. You asked why you still think about someone who hurt you. Your hand went to the one who kept going.
3. So you kept going, dear. That's the first thing I see.
4. And the ground has gone behind him, dear, and his feet still move.
5. But what happened to you was real, dear, and so is the going on.
6. That's why the two fight in you, dear. Neither cancels the other.
7. Let me look closer at what has been catching at you as you go…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of the woman you were before any of this had happened to you.
2. That is not random; you reached for the card of the self who walked in ahead of all this knowledge.
3. The Fool asks nothing of you — not to forgive it, not to forget it, not to be finished with it — it points back to the woman who walked in without knowing what it would cost, and she was not naive for that; going back to her in your mind is a different act entirely from wanting him.
4. Let me look closer at what she deserved to be told…

</details>

# `cards-wont-commit` — "Why won't he commit to me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

On the two Magician/Hanged Man/Fool decks — one set of copy, verb swapped ('turned' face-down, 'chose' face-up).

🔴 THESE ASK FOR A PREDICTION OUTRIGHT, which is the exact thing the funnel forbids, so the no-verdict rule runs in BOTH directions plus a no-timeframe rule: 'he will, give it time' reads as a promise and 'he never will' is a pronouncement on a man she loves. 🔴 The bans in tests/tarot-commitment-copy.test.ts are WHOLE-BEAT rather than clause-level, so 'not enough' and 'too much' are unavailable even inside a refusal — the usual absolution form had to be rebuilt without them.

⚠️ THIS HOOK PRESUPPOSES HIS REFUSAL, which opens a failure mode the others do not have: the answer sliding into HER fault. 123 real concerns off this lander show she has usually already started turning the question on herself — 'I feel he's never really tried. Like I'm an option even tho he said different', 'I feel inconsistent energy from him', 'he says he loves me and I believe him but there's something underlying that I can't explain'. So the 'why' is routed to where HE is stuck and left there — never a character verdict, never invented — and what gets affirmed is that her read is sound and the tiredness is earned.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — a white band round his dark hair.
2. You asked why he won't commit to you. Your hand went to the man who decides.
3. So the hold on this isn't yours, dear. Nothing you did put it there.
4. And the tools for building are all laid out in front of him, dear.
5. But having the tools is not the same as picking them up, dear.
6. That's why you get the words, dear, and the week after looks the same.
7. Let me look closer at what stands in the way of a choice…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Magician, dear — the card of intention, of the will behind what a man does and does not do.
2. You are asking why he will not, and your hand found the card that says his holding back is active rather than accidental.
3. The Magician does not name him cold or cruel — it points to a man aiming his will somewhere he has not shown you, and your read that this is a choice rather than bad timing is sound.
4. Let me look closer at where his intention has been going…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — his arms are out of sight, and his body makes a triangle.
2. You asked why he won't commit to you. Your hand went to the card that stalls.
3. So something has him stuck, dear. You've been feeling exactly that.
4. And his arms are out of sight, dear. You can't see what he's holding.
5. But where he is stuck is his to name, dear. Not mine, and not yours.
6. That's why every reason you land on slides off, dear. None of them stick.
7. Let me look closer at what has this stuck in place…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear — the card of the man who stopped mid-step and never finished it.
2. You reached for the card of suspension, and it is his that you have been living inside.
3. The Hanged Man does not say he is withholding to punish you — it points to someone stuck rather than settled, and the straight answer he keeps failing to give you is one he does not possess, not one you asked for wrongly.
4. Let me look closer at what has him stuck…

</details>

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the little dog has its tail straight up.
2. You asked why he won't commit to you. Your hand went to the one who ties nothing down.
3. So nothing in his life is tied down, dear. You saw that early.
4. And the little dog has its tail straight up, dear. It knows the mood.
5. But loose isn't the same as gone, dear. He still walks beside you.
6. That's why asking for more never turns into a row. It just slides off.
7. Let me look closer at what has been keeping this loose…

<details><summary>OLD — what she reads today</summary>

1. You turned the Fool, dear — the card of the unfinished road, of the man still treating his life as a beginning.
2. Your hand reached for the card of the not-yet, which is where he has kept living.
3. The Fool does not make him a bad man — it points to someone who has not arrived yet rather than someone who weighed you and decided against you, and that difference matters far more than anyone has told you.
4. Let me look closer at what he is still circling…

</details>
